## Administration

Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/sections/4941311407132-Administration

### Phrase Platform Organizations

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076-Phrase-Platform-Organizations  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:16Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr, edited_at

All subscriptions to the Phrase Localization Platform belong to specific organizations that form a team of people.

##### Note

For more information about Platform plans, features available in each plan and usage limits, visit [Phrase pricing page](https://phrase.com/pricing/).

[Owners and administrators](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") can view and manage the current organization's details in the Organization settings page. To access this page, either select Settings/Organization from the profile icon at the top right of the screen or use the option in the Dashboard dropdown menu at the top left of the Phrase Platform.

The Organization settings page displays information about subscriptions, payment and billing, product usage and capacity limits, SSO configuration and provides management of them through dedicated tabs:

- Subscription overview

  Contains [usage metrics](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/13872357395228#UUID-d0c73768-f31c-0510-0c3b-be5b46a76161 "Capacity-Based Pricing") for all products included in the Phrase Platform, as well as information about add-ons ([integrations](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709656492572#UUID-98e00ebc-37ac-490b-1577-e8f108fa5a9b)).

  ### Note

  Platform owners and administrators can purchase add-ons that are available on their subscription plan.
- Invoices and billing

  Contains payment and billing information, invoices, as well as options to [cancel the subscription](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/15846757282844#UUID-da3db28f-ed93-aab1-c57e-d4179e97229c "Subscription Management").
- SSO

  Allows enabling and setting up [SSO](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management") for Platform organizations.

To remove existing organizations, contact the [Support Team](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new).

##### Switching Organizations

Users can be members of multiple organizations via [invitations](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188#UUID-b4a107cc-7695-4ba9-e6ea-acd05f1dffd4 "Phrase Platform Invitations") and switch among them as required, either from the Dashboard or a particular product.

To view and switch among the organizations, follow these steps:

1. In the Dashboard, select Settings/Profile from the profile icon at the top right.

   The Profile settings page is displayed.
2. Select the Organizations tab and click on the Switch button next to the desired organization.

   The user is logged into the selected organization.

Alternatively, switch organizations from the Dashboard menu.

### Phrase Identity Management

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580-Phrase-Identity-Management  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:17Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr, edited_at

#### Platform Single Login

The Phrase Localization Platform supports single login to the different Phrase products through a single login or signup page. Go to <https://eu.phrase.com> (for EU [data centers](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5748008399388#UUID-a1a0085f-9617-12b0-b51b-bfef6f1bee4e "Data Centers")) or <https://us.phrase.com> (for US data center) to sign up or login via the Platform.

New users signing up to the Platform as well as existing Phrase users can navigate among all available products within the Platform Dashboard, without individual authentication to each of them.

Once logged in to the Platform, select the Dashboard dropdown menu in the top left corner to switch among all subscribed products as required. The product switcher is also available within each product interface for all users with a [Platform user profile](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management").

**Product access requirements**

- Access to products is defined by purchased plan
- Phrase TMS and Phrase Strings

  - User [invitation](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188#UUID-b4a107cc-7695-4ba9-e6ea-acd05f1dffd4 "Phrase Platform Invitations")
- Phrase Orchestrator

  - Access to TMS or Strings
- Phrase Custom AI, Phrase Language AI and Phrase Analytics

  - Access to TMS
  - [Administrator or Project manager](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") role in TMS

##### Note

For more information about Platform plans, features available in each plan and usage limits, visit [Phrase pricing page](https://phrase.com/pricing/).

Existing TMS or Strings users can log in through the Platform login page by entering username and password or via [Platform SSO](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-bf3cfacc-ba2d-30df-6c05-c4b20a96c5a7 "Platform SSO").

##### Important

When SSO is enabled, changing a user’s email in the UI is not supported. For SCIM-managed users, email changes are made through the identity provider.

###### Identity membership status

Users can be members of different and multiple Phrase organizations and [switch among them](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations") as required. To comply with data retention policies, users (except Owners) who are members of an organization but have no access to any products will have their personal data (e.g. name and email) automatically anonymized after 12 months of inactivity.

Each user in the Phrase Platform has an identity membership status of either Internal or External, based on how their account was created and which organization manages it.

- Internal user

  A user created directly in an organization or provisioned through SSO/SCIM. The Internal organization (home organization) fully manages the user’s identity, including profile information and login settings.
- External user

  A user that belongs to another organization as Internal and has been invited to collaborate in an additional organization. The External organization can assign tasks but cannot edit the user’s profile or login credentials.

Users can be Internal in one organization at a time (or more if in the same organization group) and may appear as External in others.

Membership status is displayed in the Users page of the Platform dashboard.

##### Troubleshooting Login Issues

In case of login issues, try these troubleshooting steps:

1. Try incognito mode

   As a first step, open an incognito window (anonymous mode of the browser) and attempt to log in. If successful, clear the browser cache, cookies, and browsing history related to Phrase sites.
2. Verify credentials

   If clearing the cookies and cache does not work, ensure correct login credentials are used.

   - If multiple accounts exist in Phrase, enter the username instead of the email address.
   - Ensure login to the correct [data center](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5748008399388#UUID-a1a0085f-9617-12b0-b51b-bfef6f1bee4e "Data Centers").
3. Reset the password

   If the password is forgotten, click Forgot password? on the login page and follow the reset password flow.

   After resetting the password, ensure the browser does not autofill login fields with outdated credentials. Confirm that the keyboard is set to the correct language to avoid incorrect input of credentials.

   The reCAPTCHA on the password recovery page may not load due to regional network restrictions. If this happens, try a different network connection, such as a VPN or mobile data. If the reCAPTCHA still does not load, contact Phrase support.
4. Switch organizations (if applicable)

   An account may be associated with multiple organizations. If logged into the incorrect one, [switch organizations](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations").

#### Platform Social Login

The Phrase Platform supports social login via the following social providers:

- Google
- Microsoft
- GitHub

Go to <https://eu.phrase.com> or <https://us.phrase.com> to sign up or log in by connecting the desired social account. Legacy TMS or Strings social accounts are also supported to log in to the Phrase Platform.

Logged-in users can connect or disconnect their Phrase profile to one of the available social providers in the [user profile settings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709742592668#UUID-850152bf-7b70-0e42-f3bd-589525cad1fc "User Profile Settings").

Social signup or login is not available for users that have been [invited to join a Phrase organization](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188#UUID-b4a107cc-7695-4ba9-e6ea-acd05f1dffd4 "Phrase Platform Invitations"):

- New users can sign up by providing their username and password or via SSO in the Phrase Platform signup page.

  Once logged in, go to the Platform's user profile settings to enable social login via the desired social provider.
- Existing users that have been invited to join another Phrase organization must restore their password in order to accept the invitation.

  Once the password is restored, log in using the existing social account connection.

#### Platform SSO

Single Sign-on (SSO) allows Platform users to log in via third-party applications. The Phrase Platform enables integrations with identity providers (IdPs) compliant with SCIM 2.0 and the SAML 2.0 protocol (Microsoft Entra ID, Okta, Google Workspace, etc.).

Users have access as long as they are logged into the organization IdP system.

By default, users can log in to the Phrase Platform via both SSO and through existing username and password credentials. If required, SSO usage can be enforced to restrict the ability to log in using username and password.

###### uniqueID parameter

The SSO login page supports the `uniqueId` URL parameter that pre-fills the Unique Global Identifier for users. Placing a string into this parameter causes the Unique Identifier field to be pre-filled for the user.

***Example***:

`https://eu.phrase.com/idm-ui/signin/sso?uniqueId=YOUR_ID`

Customers can bookmark the URL with this parameter in their browser so they don't have to remember this ID value to start the SSO login.

##### Enable SSO using SAML 2.0

SSO setup should be performed by IT administrators with admin access to the chosen IdP. Only Platform Owners can configure SSO setup in Phrase.

Upon SAML login, users' `first_name` and `last_name` are always updated in the Phrase organization user profile to match the value present in the customer's IdP when the respective attributes are present in the SAML login request. To avoid this, it is recommended to remove these attributes from the SAML login request.

To set up SSO, follow these steps:

1. Select Organization settings from the left-hand navigation menu in the Platform dashboard.

   The Organization settings page opens and the SSO tab is presented.
2. Select the SSO tab and click Enable SSO.

   SSO configuration page is displayed.
3. Fill in the Authorize SSO section:

   - Provide a unique identifier (e.g. the organization name or a random string) in the Globally unique identifier field.

     Organization users will be required to use the unique identifier when logging into the Phrase Platform.
   - Select the required option from the Identifier type dropdown:

     Username:

     Platform users are matched to IdP user identities by username. *NameID* format attribute is used to match users:

     `urn:oasis:names:tc:SAML:1.1:nameid-format:unspecified`

     Email address:

     Platform users are matched to IdP user identities by email. *NameID* format attribute is used to match users:

     `urn:oasis:names:tc:SAML:1.1:nameid-format:emailAddress`
4. Use information provided by the IdP to fill in required fields in the Configure SAML in Phrase section, then click Save.

   Fields in the Add Phrase to your identity provider section are automatically populated.
5. Copy the links provided in the Add Phrase to your identity provider section and enter them in your IdP's SAML setup.

##### Note

Signing the SAML response is required to successfully set up Platform SSO.

More information can be found in the documentation specific to the IdP (e.g. [Certificate signing options](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/active-directory/manage-apps/certificate-signing-options#certificate-signing-options) through Microsoft Azure AD).

##### Enforce SSO

Selecting Require users to sign in with SSO forces users to use SSO to sign in.

Requiring users to sign in with SSO prevents users who did not log in via SSO previously from accessing the organization.

If an external collaborator requires access outside of SSO, select the Require SSO login for specified domains option.

Options:

- Allow all login methods
- Require all users to log in via SSO
- Require SSO login for specified domains

  Provide identifiers and identifier type and click Save.

All users required to sign in with SSO are displayed in the Users page of the Platform dashboard.

##### User Auto-Provisioning

User auto-provisioning handles only company-managed users in Phrase, not self-registered users.

Phrase Platform supports two types of user provisioning using SAML/SSO features to automate access to the Platform applications:

- Just-in-Time (JIT)
- SCIM

New users are created automatically in the [Platform organization](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations") once they are provisioned access to Phrase in the chosen identity provider (IdP).

All new users are created as [members](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") of the relevant Platform organization and do not have access to any of the products by default. The Platform organization's owner or administrator will have to [invite them to the required product](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188#UUID-b4a107cc-7695-4ba9-e6ea-acd05f1dffd4 "Phrase Platform Invitations") separately.

If required, default product and role assignment can be modified when [setting up auto-provisioning](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-ac54c16d-4a89-ab5f-907f-29e8d13872cc "Set up Auto-Provisioning") in the Platform organization settings.

Created users are required to confirm their binding to the organization. To do this, an email with a verification link is sent to the provisioned user. Prior to verification, the user is not allowed to log in with [SSO](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-bf3cfacc-ba2d-30df-6c05-c4b20a96c5a7 "Platform SSO").

##### Note

To skip binding confirmation, contact the dedicated Customer Success Manager.

Provisioned users are not allowed to change their Phrase credentials, as these are managed in the IdP.

When a user is de-provisioned through the IdP and consequently removed from their last organization in Phrase, the account enters an automated 30-day retention countdown. During this grace period, the identity is not deleted immediately, allowing for restoration if the removal was accidental.

###### Set up Auto-Provisioning

To enable or disable auto-provisioning, collaboration with Phrase Technical Support is required to ensure proper configuration and prevent potential issues, such as duplicate accounts or unassigned users.

For a smooth setup and ongoing management of users, the following best practices are recommended:

1. Start with a small test.

   Before rolling out auto-provisioning to the entire organization, it is recommended to provision a small group of users to verify that everything is functioning correctly.
2. Assign all users via Identity Provider (IdP).

   After a successful test, assign all users to the Phrase application through the IdP. SCIM will then automate provisioning and user updates across the organization.
3. Convert existing users to company-managed users.

   Contact Phrase Technical Support to convert current users to company-managed users. SCIM can only update and delete users marked as company-managed, making this step essential.

   Before conversion, confirm that the email, username, first name, and last name of each existing Phrase identity match the corresponding values in the identity provider exactly. Attribute mismatches prevent SCIM from linking or updating identities correctly.
4. Assign pre-SCIM users to the organization via IdP.

   Users added before enabling SCIM auto-provisioning must be assigned to the organization through the IdP. Failing to assign them may result in their deletion during SCIM synchronization.

###### Just-in-Time (JIT) Provisioning

Just-in-Time (JIT) provisioning is a SAML protocol based method that is used to create users the first time they log in to an application through SAML SSO. This eliminates the need to provision users or create user accounts manually and all created users have automatic access to that organization's products.

JIT provisioning configuration should be performed by IT administrators with admin access to the chosen IdP.

To configure JIT provisioning through SAML SSO, follow these steps:

1. Select Organization settings from the left-hand navigation menu in the Platform dashboard.

   The Organization settings page opens and the SSO tab is presented.
2. Select the SSO tab.

   SSO configuration page is displayed.
3. Scroll down to Auto-Provisioning Configuration and select Contact support to enable the configuration settings.

   ### Note

   SAML auto-provisioning and [SCIM](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-2d39f033-df2c-9797-5bfb-90d4fb907115 "SCIM Provisioning") cannot be enabled at the same time.
4. Once the settings are enabled, select SAML Auto-provisioning (Just in time) from the Auto-provisioning dropdown.
5. Select type of Role management.

   - Default assigns access to all Phrase products with default roles upon the provisioning.
   - Custom allows selecting a role for specific Phrase products that will be applied to all new users upon the provisioning.

     If the user role is empty, users will not be provisioned to the product.
6. Use the attributes in the Attribute statements table to map attributes from IdP to data in Phrase.

   This is needed to ensure the users data is aligned between the two systems.
7. Click Save.

   Configuration is saved.

###### SCIM Provisioning

The [SCIM](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7644) protocol is an application-level standard that enables secure management and exchange of identity data across domains.

Supported SCIM functionality:

- Create company-managed user

  - The user is provisioned to all applications that are active in their Platform organization.
  - The user has Linguist role in Phrase TMS and Translator role in Phrase Strings.

    If required, default product and role assignment can be modified when setting up auto-provisioning in the Platform organization settings.
  - A SCIM-created user identity cannot be merged with an existing one. Only fresh identities are supported.
  - A SCIM-provisioned identity cannot join a different organization under the same email address unless the second organization belongs to the same customer. In that case, contact Phrase Support to request an organization group; once configured, SCIM provisions the identity to any organization within that group. If the second organization belongs to a different customer, send the invitation to a different email address, or an alias of the address, to create a separate profile for the new organization.
- Edit company-managed user attributes

  Editing attributes in the IdP is reflected in the Phrase Platform.
- Delete company-managed user

  - When the IdP sends a user deletion request, that user’s membership is removed from the Phrase organization. If it is the last organization they belong to, the account enters a 30-day retention period before permanent deletion.
  - If an SCIM-managed user is a member of multiple organizations, the deletion request from one organization will remove their membership from that organization. Only after receiving a deletion request from the last organization they are a member of will the 30-day deletion countdown begin.

[Eingebettetes Video/Inhalt](https://www.youtube.com/embed/BZHwawbkB8Q)

##### Note

Due to continuous improvements, the user interface may not be exactly the same as presented in the video.

SCIM configuration should be performed by IT administrators with admin access to the chosen IdP.

To configure SCIM properties, follow these steps:

1. Select Organization settings from the left-hand navigation menu in the Platform dashboard.

   The Organization settings page opens and the SSO tab is presented.
2. Select the SSO tab.

   SSO configuration page is displayed.
3. Scroll down to Auto-Provisioning Configuration and select Contact support to enable the configuration settings.

   ### Note

   [SAML auto-provisioning](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-f7ac6edc-292c-7263-3879-405723c4fcbf "Just-in-Time (JIT) Provisioning") and SCIM cannot be enabled at the same time.
4. Once the settings are enabled, select SCIM from the Auto-provisioning dropdown.

   SCIM configuration details are presented.
5. Enter the desired SCIM secret to use in the encoding and click Save.

   ### Note

   The SCIM secret is required due to the Phrase Platform architecture where multiple organizations can use SCIM. The organization ID is encoded in the security token to prevent the use of UID in the URL.

   The SCIM bearer token field is populated with a unique token.
6. Copy the token and the SCIM base URL.

   These will be used in the identity provider settings.
7. Select type of Role management.

   - Default assigns access to all Phrase products with default roles upon the provisioning.
   - Custom allows selecting a role for specific Phrase products that will be applied to all new users upon the provisioning.
   - SCIM allows [configuring product access and role](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-652187fd-1949-2aae-0cab-08ad5de376e3 "Configure SCIM role management") per user based on information set in the IdP upon the provisioning.
8. Click Save.

   Configuration is saved.

##### Configure SCIM role management

When SCIM is used, role assignment and product access can be defined directly in the IdP provisioning request using the `roles` attribute.

Each entry in the `roles` array of the SCIM `User Create` request must contain:

- A `type`, indicating the target product (must begin with `phrase__`)
- A `value`, specifying the exact role name for that product

**Example SCIM request**

```
"roles": [
  { "type": "phrase__platform", "value": "MEMBER" },
  { "type": "phrase__tms", "value": "ADMIN" },
  { "type": "phrase__strings", "value": "none" }
]
```

- The `phrase__platform` role is mandatory.
- Use `none` to exclude access to a product.
- All roles must match those configured and [available in the relevant Phrase product](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management"). The [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282) role is not configurable and is automatically inferred from the user’s TMS role.
- Role names are case-sensitive. Upper case spelling is recommended.
- Requests missing required fields or including invalid roles will fail.

If assistance is required when setting up SCIM role-based provisioning or troubleshooting configuration errors, contact the assigned Solution Architect.

###### Example configuration in Okta

To configure SCIM role provisioning in Okta, follow these steps:

1. Create custom attributes for each Phrase product:

   - `phrase__platform`
   - `phrase__tms`
   - `phrase__strings`
2. Define valid [role values for each attribute](https://support.okta.com/help/s/article/How-to-add-multivalue-roles-in-SCIM-Cloud-integration?language=en_US).
3. Map these attributes to the SCIM app in Okta.
4. Use conditional logic, if needed, to map roles dynamically based on user profile or group membership.
5. Apply mappings only during user creation.

   ### Note

   Updates to existing user roles via SCIM are not supported.

##### Migrate from legacy SSO

##### Important

As of March 4, 2025, the legacy SSO login pages for TMS and Strings will be deprecated. To ensure uninterrupted access to Phrase products, all organizations using legacy SSO must migrate to Platform SSO.

Migrating to Platform SSO requires administrative access to the identity provider (IdP). Collaboration with the IT team is essential to configure a new Platform SSO integration in the IdP.

Each Phrase organization can integrate with only one IdP. If multiple IdPs are used, select one for the Platform SSO setup before migration.

**Migration steps**

1. [Set up Platform SSO](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-bf3cfacc-ba2d-30df-6c05-c4b20a96c5a7 "Platform SSO") in the IdP (Okta, Azure, TrustBuilder, or other IdPs).

   - To avoid confusion for users, set the globally unique identifier in Platform SSO to match the one used in the legacy SSO.
2. Invite existing users and groups from the legacy SSO integration to the new Platform SSO integration within the IdP.
3. Communicate the login flow changes:

   - Users must use the Platform login page:

     - <https://eu.phrase.com/> (EU data center)
     - [https://us.phrase.com/](http://https://us.phrase.com/) (US data center)
   - If logging in via an IdP dashboard icon, users must select the updated Platform SSO icon.
   - User credentials (email/username) remain unchanged.

Once the migration is complete, all existing content and user roles in Phrase TMS and Phrase Strings remain unchanged.

### Phrase User Management

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900-Phrase-User-Management  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:18Z  
> Labels: Administration, User Management, 2BTr, edited_at

#### Platform User Management

The Phrase Localization Platform supports 3 global user roles with different rights to create and manage [Phrase organizations](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations") as well as product subscriptions and access.

Platform user roles are independent of the product-specific roles. Product access is managed separately from the Users page, where access and roles for each Phrase application can be configured individually. The table below represents an ideal matching of platform and product roles.

TMS is accessible only to users with an active status in TMS. Phrase Portal and Phrase Custom AI require an active TMS user profile to grant access.

**Phrase Platform user roles**

| User Role | Description | Matching TMS Role | Matching Strings Role |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| *Owner* | Creates the organization with unlimited access to all features | [Administrator](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709753812124#UUID-c512e8a2-c3ee-7321-9b76-81f0dd72517f) | [Owner](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_UUID-e0567626-50e4-3334-3b05-157f9ac0e6bb "Roles") |
| *Administrator* | Same as owner, but cannot create an owner or manage Platform SSO settings.  Can buy add-ons and top-ups, but cannot cancel a subscription. | N/A | [Administrator](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4620349063334433791573809369 "Administrator") |
| *Member* | Same as administrator, but cannot manage organizations or product trials, subscriptions and access | - [Project manager](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6) - [Linguist](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709735838492#UUID-911ee8d4-ecc1-0c38-8f71-7c22f1646545) - [Guest](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756557724#UUID-9cdd9c56-fc67-1d5f-7cf0-7fbfe3a993b9) - [Submitter](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709767598876#UUID-913b42c7-c1ba-034c-daca-75ed772677ec) | - [Project manager](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4620348684028833791574181868 "Project manager") - [Developer](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4657506687539233791574715551 "Developer") - [Designer](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4620348776003233791575061448 "Designer") - [Translator](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4612455988305633791575336531 "Translator") - [Guest](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240_bridgehead-idm4657506669059233791575674608 "Guest") |

The owner is automatically created upon sign up to the Platform. Platform Administrator and Member roles can be created within each product, or through [invitation to the Platform](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188#UUID-b4a107cc-7695-4ba9-e6ea-acd05f1dffd4 "Phrase Platform Invitations") organization and [SSO auto-provisioning](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management"). Invitations can include access to one or more Phrase products. A user can be assigned only one role at a time in each of Phrase's products.

Owners and administrators can invite both new and existing Phrase users to join the Platform organization and grant access to specific products.

###### Platform Users Page

By selecting Users from the left sidebar, owners and administrators can:

- View all users of the current organization, including their Platform role and access to Phrase products.

  ### Note

  Invited users are listed in the Pending tab and become available in the Active tab only after accepting the invitation to the Platform organization.
- Edit existing users' Platform roles by selecting the desired role under the Role column.
- Invite new users by selecting Invite users.
- View the [identity membership status](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management") of all users of the current organization in the Membership column.
- Manage product-specific access and role for existing users by selecting Manage product access from the Actions ![Open More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504394396) menu.

  ### Important

  If required, granular permissions for each product role (such as individual project, client, or resource rights) must be configured directly within the respective product interface.
- Click Permanently delete from the Actions ![Open More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504394396) menu to remove an Internal user identity across all Phrase organizations.

  - [SCIM-created](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management") user identities cannot be manually deleted from the UI. However, if de-provisioned via the Identity Provider (IdP) and removed from their last organization, the account enters a 30-day retention period before permanent deletion. Automatic removal of a deprovisioned user from Phrase depends on the identity provider sending a `SCIM DELETE` request. If the identity provider only sends a `SCIM PATCH` request to deactivate the account, the user is disabled in Phrase but not scheduled for deletion, and remains listed indefinitely. Verify the provisioning configuration with the identity provider to confirm that `SCIM DELETE` requests are sent when a user is deprovisioned. If a user remains listed as disabled indefinitely, contact Phrase support for manual removal.
  - Currently logged-in users cannot remove themselves from the organization.

  ### Note

  - If a user is removed from a specific product, they will no longer have access to that product, but the user profile will still be maintained within the Platform.
  - Users who are members of an organization but have no access to any products will have their personal data (e.g. name and email) automatically anonymized after 12 months of inactivity to comply with data retention policies.
- Click Remove membership from the Actions ![Open More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504394396) menu to remove an External user from the current Platform organization.

  - The Phrase user identity is scheduled for permanent deletion after a 30-day grace period, allowing for restoration if the removal was accidental. During this countdown, administrators may see a retention countdown status for the user.
  - If the Phrase user identity is part of multiple organizations, removing membership only revokes a user's access to the current organization. It does not delete the underlying Phrase user identity, and it does not free the username for reuse because the identity is still active in other organization.

#### User Roles (Strings)

User seats are used to create profiles for other users so that they can collaborate on localization projects. Profiles are created for product managers, developers, and translators. Each user profile has a default role, however the role can be changed on the project level. Users can also be grouped into teams.

Existing and invited users are presented on the Users tab of the Users page. Users can be sorted and clicking on the user displays role, language access, teams, assigned projects and latest user activity. Users can also be deleted from the Users tab.

##### Roles

Every user in Phrase Strings has a user role that defines access rights. Apart from the all-powerful Owner there are the six different roles. [Enterprise](https://phrase.com/pricing/) customers may have customized roles, if a custom role is required, contact the [support team](https://phrase.com/contact/).

##### Administrator

- Sees all projects.
- Can edit translations for all languages and access all features in any project.
- Can manage users and organization settings.
- Recommended for users that need full access to the organization and all projects.
- Can be promoted to owner by using the Promote to Owner function in the More menu of an Administrator.

##### Project manager

- Sees only assigned projects.
- Can edit translations for all languages and access all features in assigned projects.

  ### Note

  Project managers cannot modify [term bases](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188#UUID-ca45e09b-442e-336f-3257-2983bccd0503) in assigned projects.
- Can manage MT engines for specific language pairs in the organization settings.
- Can manage users and project settings.
- Recommended for users that need access and manage certain projects.
- Can be given global read-only access to all spaces and projects of the organization (can be activated via the user profile).

##### Developer

- Sees only assigned projects.
- Can edit translations for all languages in assigned projects.
- Can manage language settings in assigned projects.
- Recommended for users with access limited to individual projects and no administrative tasks.

##### Designer

- Sees only assigned projects.
- Can view and edit translations for all languages in assigned projects.
- Can upload files and screenshots.
- Recommended for users with access limited to individual projects, and who are responsible for design, layout, UX, etc. (such as via Sketch).

##### Translator

- Sees only assigned projects.
- Can view translations for all languages in assigned projects, but can edit translations only for assigned languages.
- Can upload files for assigned languages (if permission is given by an Administrator).
- Recommended for users with access limited to specific languages, including community users.

##### Guest

- Sees only assigned projects.
- Can view translations for all languages in assigned projects and leave comments.
- Can access term bases (can be removed).
- Recommended to give read-only access for colleagues or stakeholders not directly involved in the localization process.

##### Project-based User Roles

###### Available for

- Enterprise plan

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

###### Available for

- Enterprise plan (Legacy)

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

As the Owner or as a Admin and Project Manager, the default role of a user can be overwritten and be assigned to a project-based role. This gives specified users different powers within a project and allows teams to view unassigned projects without risk of interfering in project processes. A user, as an example, may have the default role of Developer but can be a Guest in another project.

Roles can be overwritten within the project via the Roles tab on the User page. ![Information](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682536427676) beside a user name indicates the default role assignments have been changed.

##### Teams

###### Available for

- Team, Professional, Business, Enterprise and Software UI/UX plans

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

###### Available for

- Enterprise plan (Legacy)

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

Teams are for grouping users and batch-assigning access rights. This becomes more useful the more users are working in an organization.

To create a team, follow these steps:

1. From the Users page, select the Teams tab.
2. Click Create new team.

   The Create a new team window opens.
3. Provide a Team Name.
4. In the Team members tab, click Add beside a user name to add them to the new team.

   The Add button changes to a Remove button for selected users.
5. From the Project access tab, click Add to select which existing projects the new team will have access to.

   The Add button changes to a Remove button for selected projects.
6. Click Save team.

   The new team is added to the Teams tab.

#### User Roles (TMS)

Six different roles are provided that user profiles can be attributed to and two roles that function as organizations for the [sharing of resources](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709703948444#UUID-2fb330ab-e5ad-e017-5972-a9a75c1e0360).

Individual user roles:

- [Administrator](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709753812124#UUID-c512e8a2-c3ee-7321-9b76-81f0dd72517f)
- [Project manager](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6)
- [Linguist](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709735838492#UUID-911ee8d4-ecc1-0c38-8f71-7c22f1646545)
- [Guest](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756557724#UUID-9cdd9c56-fc67-1d5f-7cf0-7fbfe3a993b9)
- [Submitter](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709767598876#UUID-913b42c7-c1ba-034c-daca-75ed772677ec)
- Portaler

  A TMS user who has access only to [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282) within a given organization. This user type does not have access to other Phrase products. Portaler users are created in Phrase Portal, in TMS, or by granting Portal access from the Users page in the Platform dashboard.

Organizations:

- Buyer
- Vendor

##### Multiple Profiles

Users can have more than one Phrase profile as long as each profile has a unique username.

When using multiple profiles, log in with the correct username and password, not email address, to access each job. Only the profile assigned to a job can access that job.

##### Note

This applies when each organization's profile has its own distinct username. For cases when a user has the same username across multiple organizations, refer to Switch Organizations in [Phrase Identity Management](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management").

##### Users Page

The Users page is accessed through either the left-hand navigation panel or clicking Users under the Administration menu in Settings ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504432668).

From the Users page, Administrators and Project managers with relevant user permissions can:

- Create or delete users.
- Bulk import new users.
- Activate/inactivate users
- Edit user details, email, role (e.g. turn a Linguist user into an Administrator), rights, set relevancy and other options.

  - For [PM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6) users, the latest 30 created jobs are listed and for [Linguist](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709735838492#UUID-911ee8d4-ecc1-0c38-8f71-7c22f1646545) users, the last 30 jobs in *Emailed*, *New* or *Accepted* status.

    ### Note

    When a PM or Administrator changes another user's email address, an approval email is sent to the user's original email address. If the original email account is no longer accessible for any reason, contact the [Support Team](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new).
- Email login instructions.
- View login history (last six months).

  - *Login history* displays logins to Editor for Web.
  - *Last login* information shown when accessing a specific user's page includes profile logins from Editor for Desktop. An export of the log will contain only the first 500 entries.

##### Create a User

**Usernames**

Usernames must always be unique to not only the organization but also a whole [data center](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5748008399388#UUID-a1a0085f-9617-12b0-b51b-bfef6f1bee4e "Data Centers"). Do not use a deleted username when creating a new user.

The maximum length of the First name, Last name and Username fields is 255 characters.

For security reasons, the **< > & % { } [ ] ^ # \* $** characters cannot be used in usernames.

Usernames should not include spaces, as this may prevent access to other Phrase products. It is recommended to prefix usernames to ensure their uniqueness.

For an existing user, a username containing spaces can prevent assigning access to Portal or Strings. When this occurs, saving the user's product access fails with a generic "missing details or invalid entries" error, without indicating that the username is the cause. Remove any spaces from the username to resolve this error and allow the access assignment to be saved.

Example:

J. Doe of ABC Organization = *ABC\_j.doe*.

J.Doe of DEF Company = *DEF\_j.doe*.

To create a user, follow these steps:

1. Click New from the Users page or click the plus ![Add New Template](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682510735772) icon beside Users in the left hand navigation panel.
2. Provide user information, Username, Role, Time Zone, role based options and Relevancy if required.
3. Click Create.

   New user is added to the list on the User page.
4. User [sets a password](https://support.phrase.com/document/preview/7928#UUID-bddb003f-dc32-06ed-f92f-75c473c51ee7).

##### Bulk Import Users

To create multiple users, an .XLSX table containing user data can be imported. A pre-formatted table is available to download to help with the import. Passwords cannot be imported.

Only [administrators](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709753812124#UUID-c512e8a2-c3ee-7321-9b76-81f0dd72517f) can bulk import users.

For security reasons, the **< > & % { } [ ] ^ # \* $** characters cannot be used in usernames.

To bulk import users, follow these steps:

1. From the Users page, click Import.

   The Import users window opens.
2. Click Download a sample XLSX file.

   The pre-formatted .XLSX file is downloaded to your system.
3. Open the .XLSX file in a spreadsheet editor.
4. Start adding users from row 4. Enter the following user information if available (if anything but the *Username* is not immediately available, it can be added manually):

   - First name
   - Last name
   - Email
   - Username
   - Role
   - Receive newsletter
   - Note
5. Save the .XLSX file.
6. From the Import users window, drag and drop the file into the window or click Choose file and select the file.
7. Click Import.

   The users specified in the .XLSX file are added to the Users page.

#### Error messages - User management

##### Active users limit exceeded (TMS)

Deactivate some existing users. Once a user account is deactivated, it does not count towards the total. Leaving the Active checkbox unchecked in a user account renders that account inactive.

![User_activation-TMS.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682510756636)

Hovering over the New button on the Users page presents balance of available users.

![balance_of_users.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682526880156)

Linguists can also be automatically deactivated if they have no assigned jobs.

To automatically deactivate users, follow these steps:

1. From the Settings ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504432668) page, scroll down to the Project settings section and click on Access and security.

   The Access and security page opens.
2. Ensure Activate/Deactivate Linguists automatically is checked.
3. Click Save.

   Settings are saved and the Settings page is opened.

### User Profile Settings

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709742592668-User-Profile-Settings  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:19Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, edited_at

#### Phrase Platform User Profile

Click the username and/or user image from the top right corner to view user profile options. When logged in, the user can see what organization they belong to, update their profile settings or sign out. Based on their [user role](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management"), they can also invite other users to join the [Phrase organization](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations") or manage its settings.

To view and manage profile settings in the Platform:

1. Select Settings/Profile from the profile icon at the top right of the screen.

   The Profile settings page is displayed.
2. Use the available tabs to manage the desired settings:

   - Personal

     Create a profile picture automatically with initials and a pictogram to generate an avatar, or upload a profile picture. Edit personal information and configure Appearance options:

     ### Note

     Username, email, first name, and last name can only be changed when the account is not managed via SSO or SCIM and the current view is the home organization. If the field appears read-only, contact the organization's Administrator.

     - Username

       View or copy the profile's username.
     - Theme

       Select to display the user interface in Light mode, Dark mode, or based on browser default settings.
     - Language dropdown menu

       Change the language in which the user interface is displayed.
     - Time zone dropdown menu

       Change the time zone associated with the user profile.
   - Security

     Manage available security settings:

     - Password

       Set up a new password for the current user profile.
     - [Social logins](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management")

       Connect or disconnect the current user profile to the available social providers to enable login via social networks.
     - [Two-factor authentication](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6999456070556#UUID-c60c70ed-e212-f8e4-c2b7-7c537daf007f "Two-Factor Authentication")

       Set up and enable multi-factor authentication (MFA). Currently, two-factor authentication via Google Authenticator application is supported.
     - Cookie settings

       Manage cookie preferences.
   - Organizations

     List, manage and switch among organizations.
   - Login history

     View and download the user login history.
   - [Access tokens](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/22675071795996#UUID-44c358c6-2265-3ec7-5ffc-b38702452a0b "Platform API Access Token Management")

     View and manage active API access tokens linked to the current user profile and organization.
3. Click Save to update profile settings.

#### User Profile (TMS)

Click the username and/or user image from the top right corner to view user profile options. When logged in, the user can see what role they have, update their profile and see their login history (dependent on user role) or sign out.

To update a user profile, follow these steps:

1. Click the username from the top right corner.

   The profile menu is displayed.
2. Click My profile.

   The My profile page opens.
3. Update User details and/or Password.

   ### Note

   As of January 30, 2026 profile picture editing is removed from TMS. Use the options under the [Platform user profile settings](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-850152bf-7b70-0e42-f3bd-589525cad1fc_UUID-609d0a5d-e534-3749-10c7-e803d974705b "Phrase Platform User Profile") to generate an avatar or update the user profile picture.

   Changing the username is only possible in [Platform user profile](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-850152bf-7b70-0e42-f3bd-589525cad1fc_UUID-609d0a5d-e534-3749-10c7-e803d974705b "Phrase Platform User Profile").
4. Click Update profile settings.

   Change are saved to the user profile.

To set or change the UI language for a TMS user, follow these steps:

1. Click Settings in the left-hand navigation menu.

   The Settings page is displayed.
2. Scroll down to the User settings section and select Language.

   The Language settings page is displayed.
3. Select the desired language from the dropdown menu and click Save.

##### Note

Editing the organization language from the Organization section in the Settings page does not change the UI language. Each TMS user must update their own UI language individually by following the steps above.

#### User Profile (Strings)

Click the username and/or user image from the top right corner to view user profile options. When logged in, the user can see what organization they belong to, view their profile settings or sign out.

To view the user profile settings, follow these steps:

1. Select Settings/Profile from the profile icon at the top right of the screen.

   The Settings/User page is displayed. Profile settings are displayed under the My profile tab.
2. Select Edit profile in dashboard to edit the available settings via the Platform user profile settings:

   - Profile Settings page:

     Personal information and login credentials.

     ### Note

     As of January 30, 2026 profile picture editing is removed from Strings. Use the options under the [Platform user profile settings](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-850152bf-7b70-0e42-f3bd-589525cad1fc_UUID-609d0a5d-e534-3749-10c7-e803d974705b "Phrase Platform User Profile") to generate an avatar or update the user profile picture.

     Changing the username is only possible in [Platform user profile](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-850152bf-7b70-0e42-f3bd-589525cad1fc_UUID-609d0a5d-e534-3749-10c7-e803d974705b "Phrase Platform User Profile").
   - Language dropdown menu:

     Language in which the user interface is displayed.
   - Time zone dropdown menu:

     Time zone associated with the user profile.
   - Theme dropdown menu:

     The user interface is displayed in Light mode, Dark mode or based on System settings.
   - Cookie settings
3. Optionally, click on Open my profile at the top right of the page to display an overview of user activity and assigned projects.

### Phrase Platform Invitations

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8604196444188-Phrase-Platform-Invitations  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:19Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, edited_at

Platform invitations and product access management can be performed only by [Platform owners and administrators](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management").

**Prerequisites**

- [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282)

  Users must have an active TMS profile. Portal access is granted as a product-specific permission.
- [Phrase Orchestrator](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/7681638082716#UUID-1cda7cbd-1aa9-d555-499b-36042949842f)

  Users must have an active TMS profile.
- [Phrase Custom AI](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/7683093364508#UUID-7e94a40d-cd95-9388-1d29-cb05cb65a32f)

  Users must have an active TMS profile and the required TMS role (Project Manager or Administrator).
- Invited users must belong to the same Platform organization for each product.

##### Invite New Organization Members

To invite new users of Phrase products that are part of the same [Platform organization](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6091807042076#UUID-de688ee0-29bf-a886-cf73-129e272a8613 "Phrase Platform Organizations"), follow these steps:

1. Login to the Phrase Platform and select Users from the navigation panel.

   The Users page is displayed.
2. Click Invite users at the top of the users list.

   The Invite to Phrase window is displayed.

   ### Note

   Alternatively, select the profile icon in the top right corner of the page and click Invite user.
3. Enter the user's email address and select the desired Platform Role.
4. In the Product access section, select the appropriate access level or role from the relevant dropdown for each Phrase product.

   - To restrict a user to Phrase Portal only, assign the Portal Member role for TMS.

   ### Important

   If required, granular permissions for each product role (such as individual project, client, or resource rights) must be configured directly within the respective product interface.
5. Click Invite.

   The invited user receives an invitation email. A new pending invitation is displayed in the Pending tab of the Users page.

   The invitation link is valid for 14 days.
6. Invited user clicks on the link in the invitation email.

   - New Phrase Platform users

     The Accept invitation page is displayed. Once all required fields are filled in, the new user logs into the Platform organization's homepage and gains access only to the invited products.
   - Users with existing Phrase Platform accounts

     The Accept invitation page is displayed. If the invited email address is already associated with a profile belonging to a different organization, the Accept invitation page presents two options: Join with an existing profile and Set up a new profile. Selecting Join with an existing profile links the invitation to the existing profile and its username, merging access into that same identity. Selecting Set up a new profile creates a distinct profile and username for the new organization, separate from any existing profile tied to that email address.

     To keep separate usernames for separate organizations, select Set up a new profile.

      After selecting the desired Platform account among the available ones, the user logs into the Platform organization's homepage.

     ### Note

     If [SSO](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800_UUID-bf3cfacc-ba2d-30df-6c05-c4b20a96c5a7 "Platform SSO") is configured for the organization and the invited user matches exactly one Platform account, the user can select the SSO option to login. Duplicate accounts associated with the same email address can conflict with SSO login or prevent the invitation from being accepted. Resolve duplicate profiles associated with the invited email address.

Existing users of a Phrase product can be invited to other products belonging to the same Platform organization and access all products through [Platform single login](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management").

Even in organizations using SSO, invitations must be accepted via the link sent in the email before attempting any login (standard or SSO). Logging in prior to acceptance will result in errors.

If an organization has automated [user provisioning](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784117127580#UUID-0ac31ba0-c689-53df-64e4-3dc9b18e7800 "Phrase Identity Management") on, user invitations are disabled not to cause conflicts between the user lists in the Identity Provider (IdP) and Phrase platform.

##### Cancel or Resend Invitations

Pending invitations that are not expired and are not yet accepted can be cancelled or resent through the Pending tab of the Users page.

- Select Cancel invite from the More options ![More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504549660) menu to revoke the pending invitation for the desired user.
- Select Resend invite from the More options ![More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682504549660) menu to send another invitation email to the desired user.

##### Manage Existing Members Access

To invite an existing user to an additional product, follow these steps:

1. Login to the Phrase Platform and select Users from the navigation panel.

   Existing members are listed in the Active tab of the Users page.
2. Select Manage product access from the Actions menu for the relevant user.

   The Manage access window is displayed.
3. In the Product access section, select the appropriate access level or role from the relevant dropdown for each Phrase product.

   ### Important

   If required, granular permissions for each product role (such as individual project, client, or resource rights) must be configured directly within the respective product interface.
4. Click Save.

   The user's access status is updated to Processing. Once provisioning is complete, the user is granted access to the selected product(s).
5. Existing user logs into the Phrase Platform through existing credentials.

   Relevant products are accessible in the Platform dashboard.

**Overview of Active User's States**

| State in the Active tab | Meaning and possible actions |
| --- | --- |
| Active | - User has already access to the product. - Invitation to the product cannot be sent. |
| No access | - User is in the same organization, but has no access to the product. - Invitation can be sent. |
| Invitation error | - The user cannot be invited to other products within the organization until the error is solved. - Contact the [Technical Support](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new) team. |

---

### Two-Factor Authentication

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6999456070556-Two-Factor-Authentication  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-06-25T10:53:18Z  
> Labels: Administration, Security, 2BTr

Two-factor Authentication, also called Two-step Authentication or 2FA, increases the security of a user profile. The profile is linked via [Google Authenticator](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.google.android.apps.authenticator2&hl=en) or another compatible application to a mobile phone. In the case of changing phones, authentication codes are linked to the Google account and will transfer automatically and if using a new account, the codes can be exported from the Authenticator application.

When signing in to a Phrase profile, a username, password and security code that is generated on the mobile phone must be provided.

Two-factor authentication has to be enabled on the user level. It cannot be enforced for all users by an Administrator.

To set up Two-factor Authentication in the Phrase Platform, follow these steps:

1. Install [Google Authenticator](https://support.google.com/accounts/answer/1066447?hl=en) application on a phone.
2. From Dashboard, select Settings/Profile from the profile icon at the top right of the screen.

   The Profile settings page is displayed.
3. Click on Security and scroll down to the Two-factor authentication section.
4. Click Generate QR code and scan the presented QR code with a phone.

   The application displays a six digit code.
5. Enter the code in the Code field.
6. Click Enable two-factor authentication.

   The profile is linked to the application on the phone.

In case of issues with the authenticator application, refer to its documentation for troubleshooting (for example, the [Google Authenticator help center](https://support.google.com/accounts/answer/185834?sjid=3268102063035723572-EU)).

### Reset Password

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/28966572450588-Reset-Password  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-29T06:24:50Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, ar_tms, edited_at

The *Forgot password* link on the Login screen restores account access when a password is forgotten or lost. Each data center has a separate Login screen, so select the correct one before starting:

- [Forgot password](https://eu.phrase.com/idm-ui/password/recovery) for user accounts in EU data center
- [Forgot password](https://us.phrase.com/idm-ui/password/recovery) for user accounts in US data center

Administrator and Owner roles cannot set, reset, or see passwords for other users.

To reset a password:

1. From the Password recovery page, provide your Username or Email and click Continue.

   Password recovery instructions are sent to the provided address or email address associated with the Username.

   If the email is not received, check the spam or junk mail folder.

   ### Important

   If multiple usernames exist for the same email address, use the username rather than the email address when resetting the password.
2. Either click the link in the email or copy it into a browser. The Create a new password page opens.
3. Provide a new password.

   New passwords must meet the following requirements:

   - Min length: 12
   - Max length: 72
   - At least 1 uppercase character
   - At least 1 numeric character
   - At least 1 lowercase character
   - Cannot be part of current email inbox name or username (case-insensitive)
4. Click Continue to change the password.

   The password has been changed, and the Sign In page opens.
5. Enter Username and new password and click Login to sign in with the new password.

The reset link must be opened in the same browser session, device, and network used to request it. If the link is opened from a different browser, device, or network, or after it has expired, the message "Link is expired or invalid" is presented. Complete the entire password reset process, from requesting the link to setting the new password, without switching browsers, devices, or networks.

#### Account blocking

For security reasons, after 10 incorrect login attempts, the message Login temporarily blocked. Try again later. is presented. Users can try again after a 5-second delay, followed by 10, 20, 40, etc, seconds up until a full one-hour delay.

If under a deadline, it may be faster to reset the password.

##### Important

**Outlook users**

Microsoft Outlook uses the [Safelinks](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/office/advanced-outlook-com-security-for-office-365-subscribers-882d2243-eab9-4545-a58a-b36fee4a46e2?ui=en-us&rs=en-us&ad=us) feature to check all links in received email. The feature invalidates the reset password link and when clicked a *Resource not found* error is presented.

If using Outlook, disable the Safelinks option and request a new reset password email. Enabling the Safelinks option after resetting the password is recommended.

### Purchasing a Subscription

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709562237852-Purchasing-a-Subscription  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-07-16T06:23:10Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr

##### Important

Information in this article is valid only for users on legacy plans as of **January 31, 2024**.

A debit/credit card, PayPal, SEPA Direct Debit (EU customers only) or wire transfer can be used to purchase a [Phrase subscription](https://phrase.com/terms/).

The following debit/credit cards are accepted:

- VISA
- MasterCard
- American Express
- Union Pay

Wire transfers are only available for the Enterprise plan and/or for payments which exceed the following amounts (upon which such option will be available in the hosted subscription checkout window):

- EUR: 4,000
- USD: 5,000
- CZK: 100,000

To pay by credit card or PayPal, follow these steps:

1. From the bottom left of Phrase, click Subscribe now

   The Your subscription page opens.
2. Select a currency.
3. Click Subscribe now on the preferred plan.
4. Provide required PM profiles and a subscription period.
5. Click Next.
6. Provide billing data and click Next.
7. Review subscription and billing data and click Checkout.

   Payment gateway opens.
8. Complete payment process to complete subscription purchase.

If changing PayPal accounts, once a PayPal account is invalid, Phrase will indicate this and provide a link for updating the account.

If payment via credit card or PayPal is not possible, the billing team can agree with the Customer, on a case by case basis, that the payment may be done via wire transfer - however, this is only available for annual subscriptions (billing cycles). For such inquiries, send an email to [billing@phrase.com](mailto:billing@memsource.com) together with the following information:

- Billing address, including a VAT number if available.
- Vendor token (found on the Setup ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682523523996) page under the Organization section).
- Preferred plan.
- Number of required Project Manager profiles.

##### Note

For more information about Platform plans, features available in each plan and usage limits, visit [Phrase pricing page](https://phrase.com/pricing/).

#### To View, Add To or Change a Subscription

Subscriptions can be managed from the Plan and billing details page as well as a listing of downloadable invoices.

To modify a subscription, follow these steps:

1. From the Settings ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682523523996) page, scroll down to the Subscription section and click on Plan and billing details.

   The Your subscription page opens.
2. To upgrade, click Upgrade plan.

   Select a plan to upgrade to and click Subscribe now. Pricing calculation is prorated.
3. To add users, click Add users.

   The current number of users is presented with the option to add more PMs. Pricing calculation is displayed and prorated.
4. To manage subscription, click Manage.

   The conditions of the existing subscription are presented but can be modified if required.

If not on an Enterprise plan, cancelling or withdrawing a subscription is also managed on the Your subscription page.

To downgrade a plan or delete an account, contact the [Support Team](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new). Downgrades can only be applied at the end of a subscription period.

#### To Update or Remove Credit Card Information

To update existing credit card information, follow these steps:

1. From the Settings ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682523523996) page, scroll down to the Subscription section and click on Plan and billing details.

   The Your subscription page opens.
2. In the Payment section, click Manage.

   A new window opens to display existing payment information.
3. Update credit card information as required.

To remove existing credit card data without adding new payment information, contact the [Support Team](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new).

### Subscription Management

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/15846757282844-Subscription-Management  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-01T06:23:27Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, ar_tms

Business Team, LSP Freelancer, and LSP Professional plans can schedule subscriptions for cancellation, restore a subscription back to active or reactivate a cancelled subscription. It is not possible to cancel a subscription within the last 30 days of that subscription as per [Phrase Terms of Service](https://phrase.com/legal/?tab=phrase-a-s-terms-of-service).

Only account **owners** can manage subscriptions.

Other pricing plans can contact their customer service manager to help with subscription management.

To cancel a subscription, follow these steps:

1. From the Dashboard, click Organization settings in the main navigation, and switch to the Invoices and billing tab. Then click Cancel subscription.

   The Cancel subscription? window is presented.
2. Click Proceed to cancellation.

   The cancellation page opens.
3. Fill out the questionnaire.
4. Read and approve the cancellation statement.
5. Click Cancel my Account on \*date of cancellation\*.

   The confirmation window opens.
6. Type the word Confirm and click Proceed with Cancellation.

   The Subscription overview tab is presented.

Click Restore subscription to reactivate the cancelled subscription.

A Reactivate subscription button will be available to reactivate a subscription for 60 days after it ends at which point, customer data will be deleted.

#### Usage Dashboard

The Usage Analytics Dashboard reflects usage of the current billing cycle and is refreshed once a day at 00:00 UTC.

- Shows product usage at the subscription level, meaning it reflects the combined usage across all organizations under that subscription, including any staging and production accounts.
- Includes forecasted usage based on consumption patterns, and provides an estimation of how usage might evolve. It is designed to help with proactive planning, helping manage spending and budgets more effectively before any overages occur.

  The forecast computation takes the last 4 weeks of use per metric, and averages out the median on a weekly basis. This is then converted into a daily rate and multiplied by the number of remaining days within the billing term.

  Although the calculation is designed to minimize the impact of outliers, such as sudden spikes or irregular activity, it is to be treated it as a guidance mechanism rather than an exact prediction.

Product usage can be tracked and understood across three key areas in Phrase, each serving a specific purpose:

- Subscription Management

  View the latest real-time consumption of each product metric up to the current moment in a billing cycle.
- Usage Dashboard

  Provides a visual overview of usage trends over time, showing how each product metric is performing against its limit during the current billing cycle.
- [Phrase Analytics](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6554447243036#UUID-4971a474-63e7-d58f-1e32-4fc570c69320)

  Offers a detailed breakdown of product usage across multiple dimensions (e.g., project, language, vendor) over the past 12 full calendar months.
