### Phrase Servers IP Addresses

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784099126044-Phrase-Servers-IP-Addresses  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-08-11T06:22:20Z  
> Labels: 2BTr

This article lists the IP addresses and domains required to configure a firewall, proxy, or allowlist so that Phrase functions correctly on a restricted network. Phrase traffic uses HTTPS over TCP port 443 unless specified differently.

This article applies when:

- Phrase connects to internal systems, such as integrations, webhooks, or MT engines hosted behind a firewall. Allow the IP addresses below in inbound rules.
- Internal systems connect to Phrase, and outbound traffic is restricted. Allow the Phrase domains below in outbound rules.

Networks without traffic restrictions require no additional configuration.

The Phrase Platform is fully co-hosted on AWS, with the exception of Phrase Studio, which is listed separately below.

#### Connections From Phrase to Internal Systems

Connections initiated by the Phrase Platform, such as integrations and webhooks, originate from static IP addresses. Allow the addresses for the region that hosts the Phrase account.

**Phrase EU**

List of IP addresses: [phrase-platform-ip-addresses-eu.yaml](https://developers.phrase.com/public/assets/files/phrase-platform-ip-addresses-eu.yaml)

**Phrase US**

List of IP addresses: [phrase-platform-ip-addresses-us.yaml](https://developers.phrase.com/public/assets/files/phrase-platform-ip-addresses-us.yaml)

To verify that a webhook request genuinely originates from Phrase, use webhook security tokens rather than relying on the source IP address alone.

#### Connections From Internal Systems to Phrase

The Phrase Platform is served through CDN distributions and load balancers that use dynamic IP addresses, so no stable inbound IP list is published. Allow traffic by domain instead:

| Domain | Notes |
| --- | --- |
| `*.phrase.com` | Covers the general Phrase Platform across multiple subdomains, for example, studio.us.phrase.com. |
| `*.memsource.com` | Legacy domain used for Phrase TMS. |
| `*.phraseapp.com` | Legacy domain used for Phrase Strings. |

Phrase domains may resolve via CNAME to AWS-managed hostnames, for example `*.cloudfront.net`. Configure rules against the Phrase domain name. The resolved targets are not stable and should not be allowlisted directly.

##### Tip

If a firewall does not support domain-based (FQDN) rules, route requests through an internal proxy server and allow the proxy's IP address instead.

#### Changes to This List

The addresses and domains on this page can change without prior notice as Phrase maintains the availability, performance, and stability of the Phrase Platform. Review this page and the linked files periodically.

---

### Setting Ticket Severity

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784092804764-Setting-Ticket-Severity  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2025-07-01T10:52:32Z  
> Labels: 2BTr

Support tickets are categorized according to the severity with the designations **P1**/**P2**/**P3**/**P4**. These designations provide guidance and are used by the Technical Support team to process tickets according to the assigned severity.

##### Important

Only **P1** severity requests that require an immediate response or direct help from Technical Support agents may be processed out of turn.

Before opening a ticket, please carry out, basic *First level* support, including but not limited to, basic problem identification and diagnosis.

When submitting a ticket:

- Classify the case according to the Ticket Severity Table.
- Do not report every issue as **P1-BLOCKER**.
- Supply detailed information so the technical support team can properly identify and diagnose the issue.

This speeds up the response times and facilitates the resolution of the ticket. Failure to provide detailed information or to incorrectly classify a ticket will result in support delays.

Ticket resolution time cannot be guaranteed for a number of reasons including, but not limited to:

- Timely end-user replies.
- Response time of a third party company.
- Level of effort - simple tickets have a shorter implementation time than complex ones.
- Engineering teams and resources availability.
- Preparation time required to release a hotfix, software update, new feature, etc.

#### Ticket Severity Table

| Severity | Definition | Approximate Resolution\*\* TIme |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **P1-BLOCKER** | Phrase as a whole or one of its components mentioned on the [status page](http://status.memsource.com) is not available and productivity has been halted. This includes situations in which custom integration with Phrase is broken and severe performance issues are experienced. The issue usually affects the majority of Phrase users and is being reported by other users.  To treat a ticket as **P1-BLOCKER** by technical support team, the distinguishing criterion is if the problem is going to be, or already is, reported on the status page\*. Technical support agents provide updates on the SP every 30-60 minutes and the submitter is informed directly in the ticket upon resolution.  If the **P1-BLOCKER** ticket severity level is used to report a non-P1 issue, the severity of the ticket is immediately downgraded by the technical support agent.  sidebar. Examples  - Saving segments in the CAT web editor is repeatedly failing for all users and an error message keeps popping up. - CAT web editor is not accessible. - Project management features are not responding or extremely slow. - Phrase is down. | 4 hours |
| **P2-HIGH** | An issue, other than P1, causes an inconvenience; a major feature is not working. Phrase as a whole, including all the components are available, but issues are experienced that have a direct impact on productivity.  sidebar. Examples  - The Alignment feature does not work. - Uploading bilingual files is failing. - CAT web editor is not accessible via our trusted network restriction feature. - Widget verification emails do not work. - WP connector doesn’t list all pages. | As commercially reasonable |
| **P3-NORMAL** | An issue, other than P1 or P2, causes an inconvenience or a moderate inconvenience; a feature or functionality does not work as expected, but a workaround is available. Phrase as a whole is operational, but the problem causes a non-negligible impact.  sidebar. Examples  - The filtering functionality is not working in CAT web editor, but works as expected in CAT desktop editor. - Complete file is not downloaded from the correct workflow in IE and Edge, however downloading works fine in Chrome/Firefox. - Linguist cannot connect to the TM when using the CAT desktop editor. - Linguist cannot log in to CAT desktop editor due to editor login settings. - Various issues with Word Count Status bar. - Job notifications get mixed up in the notification email. - Jobs cannot be exported back to the online repository. - Client cannot unshare a project. | As commercially reasonable |
| **P4-LOW** | A minor problem condition that does not affect Phrase operation such as general requests for advice on product usage, clarification on [product documentation](https://help.memsource.com/hc/en-us) or [release notes](https://help.memsource.com/hc/en-us/categories/360002405420), requests for [product enhancements](https://help.memsource.com/hc/en-us/articles/360010530340-Feature-Request-Policy-and-Process), and cosmetic UI problems.  sidebar. Examples  - UI problems - overlapping tooltips. - Highlighting a word in the CAT web editor returns the cursor in the middle of the word. - How to import an XML file? - Allow linguist users to export workflow changes - Conversation bubbles in TWE do not turn blue where they should. - How to import a Multilingual XLS file. - Wrong content imported - incorrect regex used. - Incorrectly localized UI. | As commercially reasonable |
| \*In case a client has custom integration with Phrase, the resolution time can take longer and the problem doesn't need to be reported on the status page. | | |
| \*\*Ticket resolution does not necessarily mean a fixed bug. Although an issue may be identified as a bug, resolving the bug may not be justified due to business or technical reasons. | | |

### Phrase Technical Support Policy

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784099168284-Phrase-Technical-Support-Policy  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2024-07-15T11:20:39Z  
> Labels: 2BTr

#### Scope

You shall be responsible for providing basic “First Level” support to Your Users, including the receipt of initial support requests and basic problem identification and diagnosis. In the event that You, after providing basic support, are unable to resolve technical issues, the Phrase Technical Support team shall provide You with reasonable back-up support in accordance with the following Support Policy. Our Technical Support team shall assist You and Your Users in troubleshooting and resolving specific issues relating to You and Your Users’ use of Phrase and partner with You and Your Users in the resolution of issues directly involving the Subscription Services that You and Your Users are unable to resolve. You are expected to fulfill reasonable troubleshooting tasks as recommended by the Technical Support team.

#### Support Policy

The Technical Support team is available for all paid versions of Phrase Monday through Sunday, 24 hours per day. The Technical Support team provides its services remotely, through written communication only and may be contacted through different channels of communication. You may need to grant access to your profile to a member of the Technical Support team, which can be done in the Access and Security section, in order to help troubleshoot any issues. In cases where access is not granted, We may not be able to troubleshoot the issue. We will use commercially reasonable efforts to provide support, but do not guarantee that We will fix any of Phrase’s defects or make any changes to Phrase.

#### Contacting Support

- **Help Form**

  The Phrase Technical Support team can be contacted via the Help Form located at <https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/requests/new>.
- **Help Widget**

  The Phrase Technical Support team can also be contacted via the Help Widget located in the bottom right corner of Phrase.
- **Email**

  The Phrase Technical Support team can also be contacted via [email](mailto:support@phrase.com).

#### Handling Tickets

For each specific ticket, a reference number is going to be assigned to it and the Phrase Technical Support team acknowledges receipt. If You log several different issues, We may create different Support request numbers to track each individual issue. For any reported issue with Phrase, We are going to try to replicate the issue, prepare a workaround (if available), and will make commercially reasonable efforts to solve the ticket. All tickets are categorized according to [severity levels](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784092804764#UUID-cc19ea5e-5040-902f-935f-418e81e22f5a "Setting Ticket Severity").

---

### Phrase System Requirements

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709599741596-Phrase-System-Requirements  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-03-06T08:47:44Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr

A reliable internet connection and a web browser are needed to connect to and use Phrase. Operating systems that are no longer supported by their manufacturer are also not supported.

The two most recent versions of [Chrome](https://www.google.com/chrome/?brand=CHBD&amp;gclid=CjwKCAjw_LL2BRAkEiwAv2Y3SYVRQiWB8GskddegiNY8MqQJRZhTUFVSmWS9_ScISzBYFY61vUK7DRoCz1QQAvD_BwE&amp;gclsrc=aw.ds), [Firefox](https://www.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/new/), [Edge](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/edge) or [Safari](https://support.apple.com/downloads/safari) browsers are recommended. To ensure the Phrase TMS [CAT web editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683890204#UUID-b7f5d899-ec2b-4352-a151-d45f6cfb5cd8) loads correctly in Microsoft Edge, add `https://editor.memsource.com/` to an exception list to disable [Enhanced Security Mode](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-edge/enhance-your-security-on-the-web-with-microsoft-edge-b8199f13-b21b-4a08-a806-daed31a1929d#:~:text=Make%20an%20exception%20for%C2%A0security%20enhancements).

Phrase TMS CAT web editor does not support cellphone or tablet browsers.

Application [downloads](https://phrase.com/download/).

### Data Centers

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5748008399388-Data-Centers  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-07-22T06:21:50Z  
> Labels: Security, 2BTr

Phrase currently uses servers based in two different locations: (1) a EU data center is hosted on Amazon AWS located in Ireland (eu-west-1), and (2) a US data center hosted on Amazon AWS in the United States (us-east-1).

Phrase Studio runs on Google Cloud Platform (GCP) and its servers are hosted in the following locations:

- Warsaw, Poland (europe-central2)
- Netherlands (europe-west4)
- Iowa, USA (us-central1)

Each data center operates as a separate cloud infrastructure. These are referred to as the European Union and the United States data centers and their home URLs are:

| Product | EU | US |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Phrase TMS | <https://cloud.memsource.com>  This will in time be changed from *memsource* to *phrase*. | <https://us.cloud.memsource.com>  This will in time be changed from *memsource* to *phrase*. |
| Phrase Strings | [https://app.phrase.com](https://app.phrase.com/account/login) | [https://us.app.phrase.com](https://us.app.phrase.com/account/login) |
| Phrase Platform | [https://eu.phrase.com](https://eu.phrase.com/idm-ui/signin) | [https://us.phrase.com](https://us.phrase.com/idm-ui/signin) |
| Phrase Studio | [http://studio.eu.phrase.com](http://studio.eu.phrase.com/) | [https://studio.us.phrase.com](https://studio.us.phrase.com/) |

New organizations can choose their preferred data center location during the sign-up process. After signing up, accessing a different data center requires creating a new subscription in that data center.

Note that Phrase does not provide a commitment to keep a customer’s data within a particular jurisdiction at all times, even if a customer elects a preferred data center location. Phrase operates an international business with personnel and service providers based in various different countries.

For example, Phrase Language AI makes available the services of certain machine translation engine providers, who may use their own data centers based in different locations. Those machine translation providers may not be able to guarantee that the customer’s data will remain within a particular jurisdiction. Customers looking to limit the transfer of their data to another region should only use machine translation engines with the tag Data region policy, and turn off all MT engines that do not have this data region restriction.

The service up-time and performance metrics are published on [https://status.phrase.com](https://status.phrase.com/).

For IP addresses the servers use, refer to [Phrase Servers IP Addresses](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784099126044#UUID-8406858d-acaa-f599-409c-5f8723a3fda6 "Phrase Servers IP Addresses").

For information on data encryption and security practices, refer to [Phrase Trust Center](https://trust.phrase.com).

### Data Storage

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/17019781874076-Data-Storage  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-18T06:23:02Z  
> Labels: Security, 2BTr

Once data is permanently deleted, it cannot be restored by Phrase Support.

#### Introduction

This article covers the practical aspects of data storage at Phrase. The legal aspects are covered by our [Terms of Service](https://phrase.com/legal/) and the security aspects are covered by our [Security Statement](https://phrase.com/security/).

#### Users

Once a user is deleted, the original user profile cannot be restored or reactivated. Create a new user profile instead.

#### Phrase TMS

##### Data in the Recycle Bin

Data in the recycle bin is stored for 30 days and is consequently deleted permanently. The recycle bin can also be purged immediately if required.

##### Note

The recycle bin exists in Phrase TMS only.

##### Project Storage

- Projects that are inactive (no new jobs added or current jobs not updated) for 12 months are automatically deleted by the system (a warning message will be displayed 6 months prior to its deletion on the project page). When a project is deleted, the project’s jobs, analyses, and reference files are also deleted and will be stored for 30 days in the recycle bin after which they will be permanently deleted. The translation memories and term bases selected for the project are not deleted.
- Lifetime of shared projects is controlled by criteria from the buyer's end. Once a shared project is no longer available in the buyer's profile, it also disappears from the vendor's organization.
- The [Privacy Notice](https://help.memsource.com/hc/en-us/articles/360017883799) details how other data is stored.

###### Extending the Project Lifetime

The creation of a new job within a project or the modification to the underlying job file (manually or via API) within a project extends the project lifetime.

Editing the content of a job in the [editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683847964#UUID-7174fb87-7107-2f32-ce55-5e040de5446c) does not extend the project's lifetime.

###### Project Age Definition

A project's age (how old a project is) is based on the project creation date.

##### Translation Memory Storage

With a few exceptions, translation memories are stored permanently until the end of your contract with us, unless deleted by users.

###### Deletion of Translation Memories from Expired and Unused Profiles

Translation memories are deleted from Phrase profiles that have expired for at least 6 months (the user account has not been logged into for at least 6 months while the organization's subscription remains active).

Deletion Process:

- If a profile has been expired for at least 6 months, the administrator user of the expired profile will be notified by email that the profile’s translation memories will be moved to the profile’s recycle bin.
- The administrator user can disregard the message and the translation memories will be deleted permanently from the recycle bin after 30 days. To avoid deletion, the administrator user may log into the profile, extend the subscription and restore the translation memories from the profile’s recycle bin.

For the avoidance of doubt, the deletion of user profiles and any content within the user profile after the end of organization's contract with Phrase, is governed by our data retention policies and in accordance with our [Terms of Service](https://phrase.com/legal/).

##### Term Base Storage

The policy for term base storage is the same as the policy for translation memory storage.

##### Data Backup

Phrase carries out a system-wide data backup on a daily basis. Users may back up data in the following ways:

###### Manual Data Backup

To backup essential project data, download the following:

- Original files
- [MXLIFF files](https://help.memsource.com/hc/en-us/articles/360014167260-MXLIFF-Files) (or a single MXLIFF joined file)
- Completed files

###### Automated Data Backup

Customers with API enabled plans can use Phrase [APIs](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709682579996#UUID-015b686d-9159-b677-a9d3-5960f510d43a)  to automate data backup.

#### Phrase Strings

###### Project data

Given the repository character of Phrase Strings, all project-related data is stored permanently unless deleted by users. This includes but is not limited to: Spaces, Project settings, Screenshots, Uploaded files, Orders, Comments, Branches, Jobs, OTA distributions.

Phrase Strings has no recycle bin. Content deleted by a user, including languages, keys, translations and uploaded files, is removed immediately and cannot be restored by the user. Deleting a language also removes all translations for that language. Back up data before deleting them.

A deleted Strings project can be restored on request by Phrase Support. Content inside a project (keys, translations, etc.) cannot be restored once deleted.

###### Webhooks

Webhook delivery data is automatically deleted after 30 days.

###### Notifications

In-app notifications are automatically deleted after 90 days.

###### Async downloads

Asynchronous locale file exports are stored for 3 days and then deleted automatically.

###### Data backup

We encourage users to leverage [Phrase Strings API](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6957750608924#UUID-01fc164c-7f3a-1dd8-4f98-33837d66329d) in order to regularly download essential project data:

- Locale files
- Custom metadata
- Term bases (terms, translations)

### Phrase Strings Connector (Job Sync)

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647502620-Phrase-Strings-Connector-Job-Sync  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-29T06:24:56Z  
> Labels: jobsync, 2BTr, edited_at

The Phrase Strings integration (called Job Sync in Strings interface) connects Phrase TMS and Phrase Strings. The integration supports both job-level and project-level synchronization.

- Job sync

  [Jobs](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784100517788#UUID-92182692-6284-4968-7e2d-56d1a1dee812) created and started in Strings with *In progress* status can be imported to TMS for translation, and sent back to Strings after translation. By default, jobs sent from Strings to TMS are imported as [XLIFF 2.0](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709613119516#UUID-e95395fa-bc12-6247-0a1a-9b79950fc242) files.

  Recommended for batch-based workflows with granular control.
- Project-level sync

  All keys in a selected Strings [project](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784094677404#UUID-67ae68da-d206-0281-f708-35cfdfb52252) are transferred to TMS, without requiring job creation. Newly added or updated keys become available for synchronization automatically.

  Recommended for continuous localization workflows.

**Prerequisites**

- Access to both TMS and Strings applications
- Language codes used in the TMS project must match the locale codes in the Strings job.
- The default language of the Strings project must be the same as the source language defined in the TMS project.

##### Important

[Figma](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5819515701916#UUID-c9c6bc21-3921-38e5-4339-93578f6782d7) files can be displayed in Strings as a whole live file but is not supported in TMS. The TMS CAT editor can only display translated content in a specific [Figma](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709664002076#UUID-ac361633-18bb-a420-d5c2-4e11bc726b7f) frame in SVG format.

Strings [key](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784119185436#UUID-36627f11-f180-6aef-5000-581c1517e109) translation statuses are not supported. Keys which are marked as unverified in Strings prior to the import to TMS can be returned to Strings as verified in case of unchanged target translations.

The number of [segments](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709678012828#UUID-753af50b-27d0-e386-418a-452ed30e0083) created in TMS after importing a Strings job can be different from the original number of keys in the following cases:

- Keys are marked as *excluded* in Strings. [Excluded keys](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784119185436#UUID-36627f11-f180-6aef-5000-581c1517e109) are not imported.
- The [Segment XLIFF](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c5fae93b-5710-8531-bac6-15896336765d_UUID-8895afe8-180b-47ca-e2ba-ea7a8e77a1ea "Phrase TMS Settings") option has been enabled in the File import settings for XLIFF 2.0 when creating the job in TMS. In this case, the number of segments may be higher than the number of keys in Strings.

Strings keys can contain HTML entities. When [setting up the connector in TMS](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c5fae93b-5710-8531-bac6-15896336765d_UUID-8895afe8-180b-47ca-e2ba-ea7a8e77a1ea "Phrase TMS Settings"), enable the option Enclose HTML content in CDATA to preserve HTML entities instead of displaying the corresponding characters in TMS.

Leading and trailing spaces present in a Strings key's source text are not displayed in the TMS CAT editor. These spaces are preserved in the skeleton and reinserted into both source and target when the job is synced back to Strings. This can affect character-limit validations configured in Strings, since the space count is not reflected in the TMS editor during translation.

Using the connector, multiple projects and files can be monitored in APC. Each project requires individual monitoring within a single APC.

When APC processes a Job Sync trigger, it creates one TMS job per Strings locale. The import order of these jobs is not guaranteed and cannot be configured through the project template assigned to the APC rule. No sorting mechanism is applied at any stage of this process. To apply different handling per locale group, configure separate APC rules, each pointing to a different project template.

The connector uses job locale annotations to attach metadata about the corresponding Phrase TMS project to Strings jobs and job locales. The Phrase Strings API endpoint for listing job locale annotations retrieves this metadata. The endpoint for listing job target locales also includes this metadata when the `include_annotations` parameter is set to `true`. Annotated jobs contain fields such as `tms_project_id` and `tms_in_progress`, which allow tracking of a Strings job's status in its linked Phrase TMS project.

TMS does not support using the same locale as both the source and target language in a project. For example, if a Strings job uses `en-US` as both source and target language, the `en-US` target cannot be imported to TMS, and the overall job cannot be marked as completed. Do not select the source locale as a target locale when creating jobs in Strings that will be synced to TMS.

**[ICU messages](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/7765077293852#UUID-ba78486d-af7b-f9bd-e38a-d039dea68b16)** **and [CLDR](https://cldr.unicode.org/) plural forms**

When sending Strings jobs containing ICU messages or [plural forms](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5819838743964#UUID-46fb5ff5-b818-e993-4ecc-bbd777eea828) to TMS for translation, enable the Parse ICU messages option in the [XLIFF 2.0](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709613119516#UUID-e95395fa-bc12-6247-0a1a-9b79950fc242) file import settings or in the TMS [project templates](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687).

#### Use Cases

There are a number of use cases for the connector:

- TMS Project managers can add files directly to TMS projects from an online repository.
- Set up the Phrase TMS [submitter portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709602111132#UUID-560b5531-f46e-bcd6-82d9-9a69f2af4a76) to allow Submitters to add files to requests directly from Phrase Strings.
- Use TMS [automated project creation (APC)](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647363356#UUID-3fb1230d-d6b0-83ce-cb52-98b96688b553) to have new projects automatically created when a change in the **last modified date** is detected for monitored files or a folder.

  - For both job-level and project-level syncs, the monitored project path must be selected all the way to the jobs folder (e.g. *space/project/main/jobs*).
  - For job syncs, APC is triggered by the following job updates:

    - Newly added keys or changes to existing keys
    - Modified job's due date in Strings

    ### Note

    Reopening jobs does not count as an update for APC.
  - For project-level syncs, APC is triggered by:

    - Newly added keys
    - Changes to existing keys
  - If Create a new project everytime is selected when setting up APC, the remote online repository can be monitored not only for source, but also target updates

    If choosing to monitor target updates, any changes made to the target copy in the online repository will automatically update the translation memory in Phrase TMS.
  - If Target updates made in the remote entity are automatically saved in the TM is selected, key text changes in the Strings target language are automatically updated in the TMS translation memories used in the relevant APC Project.
- The TMS [connector API](https://cloud.memsource.com/web/docs/api) can be used to automate steps otherwise performed manually through the UI. [Webhooks](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709693398812#UUID-6a1bd917-264f-438d-8d66-2e32504779ec) can be used to have 3rd party systems notified about certain events (for example, a job status change).

#### Job Syncs Page

The Job Syncs page provides a centralized view of all job sync setups (APCs) that use the Phrase Strings connector. It includes both syncs created directly in Strings and those configured in TMS, with relevant information.

From the Job Syncs page, Administrator and Project Manager users in Strings can [configure new job syncs](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c5fae93b-5710-8531-bac6-15896336765d_UUID-c0111c9a-e73d-6e77-2441-9523ca88d05e "Configure Job Syncs in Strings"), edit existing job syncs (including their active/inactive status) or delete them.

Existing job syncs can be edited or deleted by using the relevant options in the More ![More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682542871324) menu. All changes are synced between Strings and TMS. Deleting a job sync removes the corresponding APC in TMS.

The Job Syncs page is accessible both from the Integrations and the Jobs left-hand navigation menus.

#### Configure Job Syncs in Strings

##### Note

If required [custom fields](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10825060868380#UUID-7a87e704-94b0-59d4-b89c-625c8e4c1fb2) are defined in TMS, setting up a job sync from Strings is not supported.

To set up a new job sync between Strings and TMS with basic configuration, follow these steps in the Strings UI:

1. In the Integrations page, select Configure next to the Job Sync integration.

   The Job Syncs page is displayed.
2. Click + New Job Sync.

   The Create new Job Sync window is displayed.
3. Provide a Sync Name and select the Strings project(s) to connect to TMS from the Assigned Strings projects dropdown.

   Only projects with the same language combinations can be included.
4. Optionally, select the TMS project template to use. The selected TMS project template must match the set of languages defined in the assigned Strings project(s).

   Synced jobs will be added to the TMS project created from the selected template.

   - If Default is selected, a TMS project template is automatically created based on the selected Strings projects.
5. Select the project Type to create in TMS.

   - [Continuous project](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709703661468#UUID-2bd6e968-44df-ca0a-4eec-583c56016cba)

     Default option.
   - New project

     A new TMS project is created every time changes are detected in the Strings jobs.
6. In the Export back to Strings section, specify the Strings export settings to control how translations from TMS are exported back into Strings.

   - Overwrite existing translations

     Updates existing target translations in Strings.
   - Mark job as completed

     Automatically marks the job as completed in Strings.
   - Verify unchanged translations in Strings

     Keys marked as *unverified* will be automatically verified in Strings in case of unchanged target translations.
7. If required, click Advanced options to display additional export options.

   - Mark locale as completed

     Marks a specific locale as completed once its translations are synced back.
   - Create new and update existing keys

     Ensures new keys from TMS are created in Strings, and existing ones are updated.
   - Only apply translation if source matched stored version

     Prevents overwriting translations if the source text has changed in Strings since the export.
8. Click Save.

   The new job sync configuration is added to the TMS Job Syncs list with inactive status. Use the toggle under the Active column to enable the job sync.

##### Tip

For more advanced configurations, set up the connector using Phrase TMS settings.

Whenever custom mapping is used, if any change is needed, it must be applied to the connector in TMS.

Creating the Strings connector in Phrase TMS does not automatically create [APC](https://support.phrase.com/document/preview/118099#UUID-36959cde-0d01-d4c0-5475-b02f465aafe4) for automated job import/export. The APC must be configured separately in TMS.

#### Phrase TMS Settings

Some organizations already have a Phrase Strings connector created automatically, using the organization's Phrase platform identity. Check the connector list on the Connectors page before creating one manually. The auto-created connector [authenticates](https://developers.phrase.com/en/guides/managing-connectors/oauth-setup#phrase-connectors-are-the-exception) server-side using the logged-in identity, not a manually entered access token.

The connector shows the status Pending while it completes its initial synchronization after creation, then changes to Active. The Pending state is also triggered whenever any change is made to the connector configuration or when the connector settings page is saved, even without meaningful changes. While a connector shows Pending, it cannot be used as a source for job creation in Phrase TMS. Wait for the status to change to Active before creating jobs with that connector.

1. From the Settings ![Setup_gear.png](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682527086492) page, scroll down to the Integrations section.
2. Click on Connectors.

   The Connectors page opens.
3. Click New connector.

   The Create connector page opens.
4. Change the Type to Phrase Strings and provide a name for the connector.
5. Enter the account ID and [access token](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5808341130268#UUID-101fb304-22d4-0948-05a2-a58c02dce152) obtained from Strings.
6. Click Test connection.

   - If successful, a small checkmark appears next to the Test connection button.
7. Select items to import to TMS.

   If target keys are not imported, the XLIFF 2.0 file can be further segmented if Segment XLIFF is selected in the File import settings for XLIFF 2.0 files.

   - Enable or disable the advanced import options as required:

     - Apply the TMS project’s source language on import

       The TMS project’s source language is applied regardless of the *default* language in the Strings project.
     - Create a TMS job for each language

       Enable this option to prevent creating duplicate TMS jobs for locales already in progress. When enabled, changes to keys already part of an active TMS job are not reimported until the original job is completed. Recommended for more control for jobs with multiple languages.
     - Wrap HTML content in CDATA

       - The option ensures HTML entities are wrapped in CDATA to be preserved during the transfer between Strings and TMS.
       - The option allows to import content combining ICU syntax and inline HTML elements.
8. Select an export mode.

   - Enable or disable the following export options as required:

     - Overwrite translations

       Disable to prevent existing translations from being updated with the file content.
     - Create new or update keys

       Disable to prevent the creation of new keys and updates to existing keys.
     - Apply translation only if source matches

       Translations that do not align with the latest source content are excluded during synchronization. This helps maintain consistency between source text and translations, avoiding outdated or incorrect translations.
     - Verify only if translation is unchanged

       Keys marked as unverified will be automatically verified in Strings in case of unchanged target translations. When enabled, every translation returned from TMS is marked verified in Strings, whether the text was edited or just returned unchanged. Only keys whose source text changed in Strings while the job was open are excluded and stay unverified.
     - Mark language as completed

       Marks a specific locale as completed once its translations are synced back.
     - Mark job as completed

       If selected, only when the last locale is uploaded will the job be marked as completed.
9. If specified, select a default folder (only possible after saving).
10. Language Mapping is presented and can be customized if required. Ensure each Strings locale has only one mapping entry to a single TMS locale in the connector configuration. Changes in language mapping will not affect existing jobs.

    The list paginates five projects at a time with projects being sorted alphabetically; first from A-Z (capitalized) and then a-z (lower case).
11. Click Save.

    The connector is added to the list on the Connectors page.

###### Delete a Connector

To delete a connector:

1. From the Integrations page, select the connector to delete.
2. Click Delete.

   The Delete connector window opens.
3. Type "delete" and click Delete.

   The connector is deleted and the list of connectors is displayed.

##### Note

A connector cannot be deleted while it is still linked to incomplete jobs in a project, including jobs sitting in the Recycle bin. Attempting to delete such a connector returns a "Failed to delete connector" error.

Resolution: Open the project associated with the connector and complete or delete any remaining jobs. Go to Settings/Recycle bin, and empty it to remove any jobs still linked to the connector. Retry deleting the connector.

Supported in the integration:

- TMS [project templates](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687)
- TMS [workflows](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717879324#UUID-3d4fec12-4a9f-ace5-cd20-a1575786a6b7)
- Job [briefing](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784100517788#UUID-92182692-6284-4968-7e2d-56d1a1dee812) (not applicable when using project-level sync)
- Job [due date](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784100517788#UUID-92182692-6284-4968-7e2d-56d1a1dee812) (not applicable when using project-level sync)

  Due date set in Strings will update the project deadline in TMS. If there are multiple different deadlines, the furthest one will be applied to the TMS project.

Transferred from Strings to TMS as a project [reference](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-fd006a60-d1b0-dc4b-8681-00d5464cd76e):

- [Key](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784119185436#UUID-36627f11-f180-6aef-5000-581c1517e109) names

  Transferred from Strings and displayed as the context key in the *[Context note](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709700187292#UUID-c5aeb191-0174-35cf-92cc-93578f45ac8f)* pane of the TMS [CAT editors](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683847964#UUID-7174fb87-7107-2f32-ce55-5e040de5446c).

- Key descriptions

  Transferred from Strings and displayed as the context note in the *Context note* pane of the TMS CAT editors.

- Max. key character length

  Transferred from Strings and displayed within the *Context note* pane of the TMS CAT editors.

- Screenshots

  To display screenshots from Strings in the Context note pane of the TMS CAT editors:

  - Enable the option Allow loading of external content in Phrase CAT editor under the [Access and Security](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709585565980#UUID-424b8fbe-2d60-48eb-891a-a4f1c5004c82) section of TMS project settings.
  - Select Rich text format in the Context note pane.
- [Custom metadata](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/11405341129628#UUID-21afd27c-d84f-5b2a-405b-ab7523497e2f) in supported file formats:

  - .XLSX
  - .CSV
  - .XLIFF 2.0

#### Monitoring Target Updates made in Strings via APC

To monitor target updates made in Strings via APC, follow these steps:

1. When setting up a APC, select Create a new project everytime.

   This enables the monitoring of the online repository for target updates made in Strings.
2. In the Automations section of the APC setup, select Target updates made in the remote entity are automatically saved in the TM.

Changes made to the target language content in Strings will be automatically saved to the Phrase TMS translation memory when the APC monitoring is triggered.

It is possible to overwrite the translation memory updates by modifying the target segments in Phrase TMS editor. These updates will be exported back to Strings when the Phrase TMS job is completed.

##### Key Deletion Behavior with APC

Deleting a key in Strings after it has been included in a job does not affect a Phrase TMS job that was created through a one-off manual import. That job remains unchanged until it is manually re-imported.

When APC monitoring is active, Phrase compares a content hash on each poll or webhook trigger and treats any change, including a key deletion, as an update that triggers a resync.

- In continuous project mode, the resync updates the existing Phrase TMS project or job to match the current content in Strings, so the deleted key's content is removed from that job.
- In create a new project every time mode, the resync creates a new Phrase TMS job version reflecting the post-deletion content, while previously created job versions remain unchanged.

#### Job Sync Troubleshooting

The table below outlines common issues that users may encounter when using the Strings integration. It provides possible causes and solutions for troubleshooting.

| Issue | Cause(s) | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Warning message related to `NMTOKEN` in TMS jobs | Key names contain undesired characters (e.g. spaces) | Users can still handle translation content and export it back to Strings, despite the warning message. |
| Keys in TMS are empty | Job contains keys without source text in the source language | Either:  - Ignore the segments. - Remove the keys from the Strings job and re-import the source from the online repository. |
| APC is not creating projects/processing new jobs | Language codes mismatch | Ensure that language codes in Strings match those in the TMS project.  When APC is set as a continuous project, any changes made to the project template after project creation will not be applied to existing projects. Therefore, changes must be made directly to the continuous projects. |
|  | APC considers the job/locale as processed | If project template/APC settings are modified after starting the job, it may be considered as processed in the APC history. Therefore, an update to the job may be necessary to qualify it for the next iteration.  Either:  - Add a new key to the job, or update an existing key of the job - Change the job's due date in Strings |
|  | `429 Too many requests` error (Strings API [limit](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/8548271212188#UUID-f8f71705-f128-f69d-8114-25c607e6fa1e)) | Reduce the number of APCs and lower the monitoring frequency. |
| Existing key in the Strings project is missing in the TMS job | The specific key was not added to the Strings job | Add key to the Strings job and reimport job from online repository in TMS |
| "Could not authenticate" error appears when trying to import/export jobs. | Expired or revoked access token on the auto-created "created by Phrase Strings" connector | Create a new Phrase Strings connector in TMS and re-link existing APCs to the new connector and trigger the monitor. |
| Strings job cannot be found for import or re-import via APC or manually, even though it is not shown as completed. | The job was already imported into a TMS project that was later moved to the recycle bin without being completed or purged. Because the project is technically still in progress, the job isn't released for re-import. | Check the job's Annotations next to its locale in Strings to verify whether it has already been imported into TMS before troubleshooting further. If the linked project is found in the recycle bin, either restore the project and continue working on it directly, or, if the project is not needed, cancel the jobs and project, or permanently delete the project via the API using the `purge=true` parameter so APC no longer considers it an active version. |
| Job Sync fails to save, with the "`Locales are not supported by Phrase TMS: x`" error, even though the TMS connector has a custom locale mapping configured. | Job Sync validates target locales in the Strings project directly against the list of [locales natively supported by TMS](https://support.phrase.com/document/preview/70696#UUID-4059dde1-081e-7eca-e826-cc39b229b719) at save time. This validation does not account for custom connector locale mappings. | For a Strings project with a target locale not natively supported by TMS, use the Strings connector directly in TMS instead of from Strings and configure custom language mapping. |
| Translations are not exported back to Strings even though jobs show a *delivered* status in TMS. | The project or template has a project status automation setting that marks the project (and its jobs) as completed/delivered once all jobs are pre-translated, rather than once they are delivered. This forces jobs into delivered status without running the export step to Strings, so the automation service never schedules the export and no translations are sent back to Strings. | In the project template's *Project status automation* settings, set Mark project as 'completed' once all jobs are" to "delivered in all their workflow steps instead of completed in all their workflow steps.  For jobs created manually, manually export the job back to Strings and manually change the job status to *delivered*, since manual exports do not update the job status automatically. Once all jobs in the project are set to *delivered*, the project is automatically marked as *completed*. |

### Phrase Service Status Page

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709594731036-Phrase-Service-Status-Page  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2024-08-28T09:55:46Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr

The status of Phrase servers and services are displayed on the [Phrase Status Page](https://status.phrase.com/), including information about uptime, downtime and outage. In the case of a suspected service disruption, refer to this page first.

To activate a subscription informing when incidents occur and when they are resolved with a postmortem document, click SUBSCRIBE TO UPDATES. Subscriptions can be specific to an incident.

Subscriptions can be in the form of email, RSS, Atom or Twitter feeds.

### Phrase Access Speed

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709599796892-Phrase-Access-Speed  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2025-08-14T13:38:49Z  
> Labels: Administration, 2BTr

Slow response time from Phrase can be caused either by problems on the Phrase side or by an internet connection or VPN.

If experiencing slow response times, follow these steps:

1. Check the [Phrase status](https://status.phrase.com/) page to see if there are any incidents reported and to see the Web App - Response Time. If the response time is less than 500 ms, Phrase is working correctly. If the response time is higher, [contact the technical support team](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784099168284#UUID-f8b698c8-d481-e1bc-d622-a04a2ca18d1a "Phrase Technical Support Policy").
2. Verify access to Phrase servers with [host tracker](https://www.host-tracker.com/en/ic).
3. In many cases, the problem is caused by an Internet Service Provider re-routing the connection to Phrase servers via too many other servers, generating slow response times.

   If working with Windows, download an application for tracking connection routes such as [WinMTR](https://sourceforge.net/projects/winmtr/) or similar. Generate a log and discuss it with the provider.
4. Use of VPNs can slow down or disrupt response times from Phrase and render the platform unusable. There is nothing specific to Phrase that should affect access via VPN so if encountering issues, contact your VPN provider first.

### Phrase QPS Overview

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709672289180-Phrase-QPS-Overview  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-03T06:23:08Z  
> Labels: Machine Translation, 2BTr, ar_tms

#### Phrase Quality Performance Score (QPS)

Phrase QPS is an AI Quality Estimation system intended to predict scores derived from the industry-standard Multidimensional Quality Metrics ([MQM](https://themqm.org/)) framework. It takes both source text in the original language and the translated target text (from MT or otherwise) and provides in response a score ranging from 0 to 100 reflecting what it believes the translation would score were it reviewed by a human using the MQM evaluation framework.

Phrase QPS is available for all editions of Phrase TMS , Phrase Language AI via API, and Phrase Strings.

#### Phrase TMS implementation

- Phrase QPS replaces MT Quality Estimation in all places where this feature was previously available.
- QPS scores appear at the segment level together with other translation resources (TM, NT, TB). Match origin is presented in a tooltip and at the bottom of the CAT panel in the metadata section if the Display Phrase Quality Performance Score matches in Phrase CAT editor option is selected in the [project settings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10825091328028#UUID-1b4ea28a-d66f-0cf7-6a75-f04f8dc61a3e).
- If enabled, QPS scoring is displayed in the [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282) translation interface to evaluate the MT quality of file translations.
- A dedicated dashboard in [Phrase Analytics](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6554447243036#UUID-4971a474-63e7-d58f-1e32-4fc570c69320) helps find the optimal QPS threshold to balance cost savings and quality risks.
- Previously MTQE scores were only available in four categories: 0, 75, 99, 100. Phrase QPS uses integer values ranging from 0 to 100.

[Eingebettetes Video/Inhalt](https://www.youtube.com/embed/a59geTpujy4)

##### Note

Due to continuous improvements, the user interface may not be exactly the same as presented in the video.

#### Phrase Language AI via API

- Users will have access to document-level scores via the API, which will enable them to quickly identify the quality of a translated document.

#### Phrase Strings Implementation

- QPS scores are added to the editor.
- When a translation is saved, Strings automatically assigns a score of 0-100.
- Scores will help translators identify where to target their attention for higher-quality output.
- Users will be able to toggle on and off the option to display QPS scores in the editor.

[Eingebettetes Video/Inhalt](https://www.youtube.com/embed/ItQxyXTSR8E)

##### Note

Due to continuous improvements, the user interface may not be exactly the same as presented in the video.

##### Phrase Orchestrator Implementation

- QPS endpoint is available as an action to create workflows that automatically route content based on QPS scores:

  - Users can create TMS workflows and define which [segments](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709678012828#UUID-753af50b-27d0-e386-418a-452ed30e0083) should be sent for human review based on specified QPS score.
  - Users can create TMS workflows and define which group of segments should be sent for [LQA](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/27529737766044#UUID-8c8a9e28-99dd-f432-f780-331192127c69) based on specified QPS score.
  - Users can create Strings workflows to evaluate [keys](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784119185436#UUID-36627f11-f180-6aef-5000-581c1517e109) for quality, which can then be routed according to user-defined decision criteria.

[Eingebettetes Video/Inhalt](https://www.youtube.com/embed/dke9mDwPrOQ)

##### Note

Due to continuous improvements, the user interface may not be exactly the same as presented in the video.

#### Quality Scores

Scoring categories:

- 95 - 100: Excellent

  The highest level of translation quality. Excellent translations are fluent, accurate and stylistically appropriate.
- 90 -94: Very good

  A high-quality translation with limited room for improvement. Very good translations capture the content of the source and articulate it well in target.
- 75 - 89: Good

  A good translation of source content, with room to improve the fluency and stylistic expression of the target text.
- 0 - 74: Poor

  A poor translation may fail to communicate source content with sufficient accuracy and clarity.

### Academic Plan Description

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/18932312255004-Academic-Plan-Description  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:31Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, edited_at

Institutions that qualify for a free license of the Phrase Platform under the academic program are granted access to the Academic plan.

The Academic plan comes with specific product capabilities and usage limits. Phrase Academy is not included in the Academic plan.

Available product capabilities:

- [Phrase TMS](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/categories/5709486381084)
- [CAT editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683847964#UUID-7174fb87-7107-2f32-ce55-5e040de5446c)
- [Phrase Strings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/categories/4930564750748)
- [Phrase Language AI](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709660879516#UUID-b0d64b4f-fa01-f7c3-006a-40e0ee0dedd2)
- [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282)
- [Phrase Analytics](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6554447243036#UUID-4971a474-63e7-d58f-1e32-4fc570c69320)

| Usage Limits |  |
| --- | --- |
| **Phrase TMS Seats**  The number of individual User accounts registered within the Phrase TMS application and designated as active. | unlimited |
| **Phrase Strings Seats**  The number of individual User accounts registered within the Phrase Strings application and designated as active. | 150 |
| **Phrase TMS Processed words**  The number of [words processed](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/14409846225308#UUID-686ee438-7db4-5f6f-7f83-ff41950483a2) by the Customer for translation within the Phrase TMS application. | 6,000,000 annually\*  \*capacity resets upon renewal |
| **Phrase Strings Managed words**  The number of [words stored](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/13381554813596#UUID-431263fa-b80f-34c7-d5ce-49c660dfef95) by the Customer at any point in time within the Phrase Strings application. | 500,000 |
| **Machine Translation Units (MTUs)**  MTUs quantify Customer’s usage of Phrase Language AI based on our [calculation methodology](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/11530492252444#UUID-3c8ba1b3-290a-5451-ba45-71797af5a755). MTUs represent the number of units used by the Customer through its use of Phrase Language AI. | 3,000,000 annually\*  \*capacity resets upon renewal |
| **Phrase Strings MT characters**  Phrase Strings offers machine translation from Google, Microsoft, Amazon, and DeepL. | 100,000 monthly  MT characters are reset every 30 days from the day the account was created. |
| **Phrase Portal Users**  The number of individual users in Customer’s organization with access to the Phrase Portal. | 5 |

Capacity is added as per regular [platform pricing](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/13872357395228#UUID-d0c73768-f31c-0510-0c3b-be5b46a76161 "Capacity-Based Pricing").

#### Exceeding Academic Plan Capacity Limits

If an organization reaches or exceeds any pricing metric capacity, the feature that has reached its limit may become restricted across that organization. The [account administrator](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management")(s) will be notified when an organization reaches the following capacity:

- ***When capacity has reached 80% of the plan limit.***

  At 80%, the organization will still have full access to all the plan's features. We encourage adding capacity or upgrading the plan to continue working uninterrupted.
- ***When capacity has reached 100% of the plan limit.***

  At 100%, the plan’s full capacity has been reached and the organization will be required to upgrade the plan or add capacity to continue working uninterrupted.
- ***When capacity is reached or exceeded.***

  Functionality and usage of features will be restricted. To regain full access, the organization will need to add capacity to the pricing metrics that have exceeded the capacity limits or upgrade the plan.

The following functionalities will be restricted when capacity is reached or exceeded:

- TMS Processed Words Capacity

  - Capacities are measured and consumed within one billing cycle and reset automatically at the end of each billing cycle.
  - Depletable capacities are adjusted for the billing cycle frequency.
  - Job imports:

    Importing jobs via the UI, API, or continuous localization features will create "ghost" jobs. These jobs will have no word count and cannot be downloaded. Jobs that have this status will need to be imported again after restrictions are removed.
  - Submitter Portal:

    Submitting projects through the Submitter Portal will be restricted.
- [MTU Capacity](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/11530492252444#UUID-3c8ba1b3-290a-5451-ba45-71797af5a755)

  - Capacities are measured and consumed within one billing cycle and reset automatically at the end of each billing cycle.
  - Depletable capacities are adjusted for the billing cycle frequency.
  - Machine translation:

    Will not be available in TMS analyses, pre-translation, and MT suggestions in the CAT editor.
- Strings Managed Words Capacity

  - Pricing Metrics with non-depletable capacities are not reset at the end of each billing cycle and can be increased at any point (or decreased).
  - Creating new projects

    Creating new projects through the UI and API will be restricted.
  - Updating existing keys

    Updating existing keys through the UI and API will be restricted.
  - Adding new keys

    Adding new keys and uploading files through the UI and API will be restricted.
  - Read-only access to all data will still be available as well as the ability to export languages and delete projects, languages, keys and jobs.

### How to Get the Academic Plan

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5881369075612-How-to-Get-the-Academic-Plan  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:24:32Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, edited_at

The Academic plan is the Phrase-sponsored plan offered to academic institutions with translation programs. It can only be used for student training and not for commercial purposes.

The person setting up the Academic plan must be a teacher or another official representative of the academic institution where the translation program takes place.

#### Setting up the Academic Plan

To setup the Academic plan, follow these steps:

1. Create a [free trial](https://eu.phrase.com/idm-ui/signup?uiLang=en-US&_gl=1*h6f8ch*_ga*NjM1MjYzNTQwLjE2Njk5NzIyMDE.*_ga_J2EQJSNGG3*MTY5NjgzODIzMS4yNDEuMC4xNjk2ODM4MjMxLjAuMC4w).
2. Publish an acknowledgement that you have been granted free access in your translation courses. Follow the acknowledgement instructions in this [PDF](https://drive.google.com/file/d/15AZj2GMJXuv9xx48zfNUoFUK60j06foB/view?usp=sharing).
3. Contact [our Academic team](mailto:academic@phrase.com) to convert your trial into a regular academic subscription. Provide a link to the acknowledgement and your Organization ID number.

   The Organization ID number is found by clicking on Settings/Profile/Organization and hovering over the information tooltip ![Information](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682536833308).

The Academic plan will be activated for a 12 month term but upon request will be extended free of charge. Contact [the Academic team](mailto:academic@phrase.com) to request an extension.

#### Getting Started with the Academic Plan

This article is specific to the Academic plan.

If you have any suggestions or if you need any assistance, please contact [our Academic team](mailto:academic@phrase.com).

There are three suggested ways of working with the Academic plan that depend on how the use of translation and project management software is going to be taught. Choose the one that suits you best.

- [Translation](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c1b5c3c4-819d-e322-f273-6ef55e304414_UUID-38972765-c66d-d1b8-d9bf-b5b39fe20ea3 "Translation")
- [Translation and project management](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c1b5c3c4-819d-e322-f273-6ef55e304414_UUID-7620f696-5e40-59a6-767a-1b3edbbf356a "Translation and Project Management")
- [Simulated agency](https://support.phrase.com#UUID-c1b5c3c4-819d-e322-f273-6ef55e304414_UUID-4eec0471-c8f3-1b1b-7337-0f851ca89dd6 "Simulated Agency")

##### Translation

- TMs and TBs are not shared so only the effort of the individual student creates value and is measured.
- As many jobs as required can be added to a project. With every job performed, TMs and TBs grow to leverage earlier translation work performed by that student.
- If there are more lecturers in your translation department and you want them to manage their own groups of students ,create a PM profile for each of them.

To setup Academic plan for translation education, follow these steps:

1. [Create](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") a [Linguist](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709735838492#UUID-911ee8d4-ecc1-0c38-8f71-7c22f1646545) profile for each student. If prepared in a spreadsheet, [bulk import](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") of users can be used.
2. [Create a project](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-fd006a60-d1b0-dc4b-8681-00d5464cd76e) for each student. A [project template](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687) may be created first and used for all students.

   The project naming convention *Project [**Student Name**]* is suggested.
3. Create a [TM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75) and a [TB](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188#UUID-93902efd-b7e0-2dc0-8032-6643b2f369a8) for each student. If externally prepared, these can be imported to a created [TM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709739829532#UUID-1d5509ec-f537-4aff-cede-ce93f80b713c) or [TB](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733407772#UUID-ab1228fd-1eaf-b83e-1f33-cb7673629755).

   The naming convention *TM [**Student Name**]* and *TB [**Student Name**]* is suggested.
4. From the [project page](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-fd006a60-d1b0-dc4b-8681-00d5464cd76e), assign TMss and select TBs for student projects.
5. [Create jobs](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709686763420#UUID-33f88350-daa8-2ab2-e7ce-e35300ec60f8) in the projects for each student.
6. As students complete their assignments (jobs), change the status of the job to *Completed by Linguist*.
7. Completed assignments can now be downloaded from individual student's projects.

##### Translation and Project Management

- Students create and maintain their own [projects](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-fd006a60-d1b0-dc4b-8681-00d5464cd76e), [TM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75)s, and [TB](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188#UUID-93902efd-b7e0-2dc0-8032-6643b2f369a8)s. These resources are only visible to the student who created them.
- TMs and TBs are not shared so only the effort of the individual student creates value and is measured.
- As many jobs as required can be added to a project. With every job performed, TMs and TBs grow to leverage earlier translation work performed by that student.

To setup Academic plan for translation and project management education, follow these steps:

1. [Create](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") a PM profile for each student. If prepared in a spreadsheet, bulk import of users can be used.
2. Set the following [user rights](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6) for each student:

   - Create projects
   - Create project templates
   - Create TMs
   - Create TBs
   - Create users
   - Create clients, domains, subdomains
   - Create vendors
   - View data owned by the user
3. Have students [create their own projects](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-fd006a60-d1b0-dc4b-8681-00d5464cd76e).

   The project naming convention *Project [**Student Name**]* is suggested.
4. Have students create a TM and a TB. If externally prepared, these can be imported to the created TM or TB.

   The naming convention *TM [**Student Name**]* and *TB [**Student Name**]* is suggested.
5. From the project page, have students assign their TMs and select their TBs.
6. [Create jobs](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709686763420#UUID-33f88350-daa8-2ab2-e7ce-e35300ec60f8) in the projects for each student. This can be done by yourself as the educator, or files can be sent to the students to be uploaded as jobs.
7. As students complete their assignments (jobs), change the status of the job to *Completed by Linguist*.
8. Completed assignments can now be downloaded from individual student's projects.

##### Simulated Agency

- The license can be used to setup a simulated translation agency. Educators or students can create projects, TMs and TBs and assign jobs to translators, reviewers, etc.. Assigned jobs enter a specified workflow and notifications are sent to defined users.
- It is recommended that the same text is not translated more than once in the same language combination. Translated texts are saved in the TM and no translation work will be required if replicated.
- The administrator is able to monitor all processes going on within the license.
- Work with any language pair that your colleagues and students speak. If there are pairs of students that speak the same foreign language, make them translator–reviewer pairs and let different pairs translate the same source text to various target languages.
- Roles can be changed. The profiles do not have to be assigned to specific names so students may try different roles.
- Use deadlines to inform students when assignments should be finished.
- Lecturers from the Maastricht School of Translation and Interpreting have prepared a network of simulated agencies. Visit [their website](http://www.instb.eu/) if interested.

To setup Academic plan for virtual translation agency, follow these steps:

1. Create [PM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6) profiles for those who will manage projects. These can be either lecturers or students that are already familiar with basic functionality.

   To create departments within your license, use [business units](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709562165916#UUID-31acda01-ba84-069d-e857-37efba29666c). This allows some projects, TMs, and TBs to be visible only to users within the selected unit.
2. If more than the standard translation, revision, and client revision steps are required, create more [workflow](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717879324#UUID-3d4fec12-4a9f-ace5-cd20-a1575786a6b7) steps.
3. Have PM students [create user profiles](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-e2b3dca6-b062-9f47-48a0-c3502f7af240 "Phrase User Management") for translators, reviewers, etc.. Users can have multiple roles and can be either students or educators.

##### Note

Special thanks to Mr. Joop Bindels from the Maastricht School of Translation and Interpreting and Ms. Maria Fernandez Parra from the Swansea University for sharing how they set up these simulated agencies.
