### AI Translation Agent

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/20660272640284-AI-Translation-Agent  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-28T06:00:59Z  
> Labels: 2BTr, AI, edited_at

#### Available for

- Team, Professional, Business, Enterprise and Software UI/UX plans

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

The AI Translation Agent is a GenAI-powered agent that produces context-aware translations with built-in review and refinement. It streamlines and improves translation workflows.

Unlike traditional setups that use multiple disconnected AI components, AI Translation Agent integrates multiple capabilities into a single component, managing the process end-to-end and minimizing the need for manual review of intermediate steps.

The AI Translation Agent combines GenAI with resources like TMs, TBs, and [style guides](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/25124298060060#UUID-bf4127ed-c920-47ac-3b5e-e80d6b1044af). If an organization switched to [content groups](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/28818060694812#UUID-7cdd8089-2074-3361-8c9e-cc708632fc05), it draws rules from the content group rather than the style guide as those are only used as the source for extracting those rules, not directly in translation. The AI Translation Agent can leverage the style rules of only the content group that a project is assigned to. When content is automatically translated, it goes through a full agentic pipeline: translation, error correction, terminology handling, and fluency refinement without any additional configuration.

AI Translation Agent leverages multiple large language models and performs the following tasks:

- Context-aware translation across multiple segments
- Dynamic real-time customization through [retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Retrieval-augmented_generation), using [translation memories](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75) attached to projects
- Refinement for consistency and fluency
- Fixing of linguistic errors
- Terminology correction based on [term bases](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188#UUID-ca45e09b-442e-336f-3257-2983bccd0503) attached to [projects](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-30234837-9796-c6f2-1a21-a623b67ac6cc)

  When a term base is assigned to the project, AI Translation Agent respects *preferred* and *forbidden* terms defined in the term base to guide the generated output. Other term base information is not currently used.
- Correction of [tag](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709695024412#UUID-9f36c0c5-d7f2-0f81-ec55-44efc63d0050) errors

#### Usage costs

Customers do not need their own LLM credentials to use AI Translation Agent. Phrase handles token consumption and normalization. Usage is calculated from the number of target words.

AI Translation Agent usage is priced in [AI Units (AIUs)](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/14032731809052#UUID-cc9cfa45-5b73-500c-edfc-a3b77c44713f) on capacity-based plans, and in credits on credit-based plans.

Example of a [pre-translation](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717749788#UUID-a982e5f9-9aa2-434f-116c-17b822d35343) configuration where 101% matches are locked, and TM threshold is 90. A job has a total of 100 words across 10 segments.

- 1 segment (10 words) comes from TM as 101% match, and is locked.

  No AIUs, MTUs or credits consumed, because the segment comes from the TM.
- 1 segment (10 words) comes from TM as 100% match, and is unlocked.

  No AIUs, MTUs or credits consumed, because the segment comes from the TM.
- 1 segment (10 words) is a 90% fuzzy match from TM.

  No AIUs, MTUs or credits consumed, because the segment comes from the TM.
- 1 segment (10 words) is a 99% match from Non-Translatable.

  No AIUs, MTUs or credits consumed, because the segment is a non-translatable.
- 6 segments (60 words) are new, empty targets, with no suitable TM or NT available.

  AIUs/credits are consumed for 60 words.

#### Using AI Translation Agent

AI Translation Agent can be selected directly from Phrase TMS [projects](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-30234837-9796-c6f2-1a21-a623b67ac6cc) and [project templates](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687), Phrase Strings [machine translation settings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5821202515996#UUID-3f860272-82b6-b342-44a0-a97106ae0685), [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282), Phrase [Studio](https://support.phrase.com/document/preview/130377#UUID-422b0d77-4832-6a6d-1a73-82bf1f2bd3b0), and [API](https://developers.phrase.com/en/api/language-ai/list-language-ai-profiles/list-language-ai-profiles) to translate and improve content.

##### Note

Click Enable multiple profiles in Phrase Language AI to make the AI Translation Agent selectable in Phrase TMS and Phrase Portal.

- **Phrase TMS**

  - Projects

    Select AI Translation Agent in the AI/machine translation service settings when [creating or editing a project](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10825044285340#UUID-3ec95012-fe40-e725-1b2e-67b8d303676d).

    The system will use it to [pre-translate](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717749788#UUID-a982e5f9-9aa2-434f-116c-17b822d35343) content in the [CAT editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683847964#UUID-7174fb87-7107-2f32-ce55-5e040de5446c) with the specified tone and style, while leveraging any [translation memories](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75) (TMs) and [term bases](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188#UUID-ca45e09b-442e-336f-3257-2983bccd0503) (TBs) attached to the project.
  - Project templates

    Select AI Translation Agent in the AI/machine translation service settings when [creating or editing a project template](https://support.phrase.com/document/preview/2707#UUID-345fe772-109d-601a-276e-c605512bd1e7).
- **Phrase Portal**

  Select AI Translation Agent in the Languages and resources settings when creating or editing a portal.

  The system will use it to generate translations in the translation interface of the portal, while leveraging any translation memories (TMs) assigned to the portal itself.
- **Phrase Studio**

  - To set AI Translation Agent as the default engine for all new projects, select it as the Translation engine in Preferences under Settings.
  - To use AI Translation Agent for a specific project, select it as the Translation engine in the project's Settings.
- **Phrase Strings**

  Select AI Translation Agent as the default AI/machine translation service, or set it individually per language pair in the organization's [machine translation settings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5821202515996#UUID-3f860272-82b6-b342-44a0-a97106ae0685). AI Translation Agent supports translation memories and term bases from both Phrase TMS and Phrase Strings.

#### Improving translation consistency

Rules are provided to AI Translation Agent as contextual input for the underlying language model. Adherence to individual rules is probabilistic by nature, and no configuration guarantees that a rule is followed in every segment.

To improve consistency, critical rules can be written explicitly as mandatory, for example, by adding wording such as "This is a hard rule, make no exceptions." Rules framed this way receive higher priority from the model.

For more consistent terminology adherence, a term base can be used instead, or in addition to, a style guide rule. Adding the correct form as a preferred term and an incorrect form as a forbidden term gives AI Translation Agent a stronger signal than a style guide instruction alone. Term bases are designed to handle terminology at scale and are the recommended place for the bulk of terminology. Rules are better suited to a limited number of terms that require contextual conditions, for example: "Translate 'back' as X only in the context of back pain." Adding a rule stating "prioritize terminology over fluency" also helps as AI Translation Agent prioritizes fluency by default, which means terminology requirements may be deprioritized when they conflict with producing natural-sounding output.

#### AI Translation Agent Insights

##### Available for

- Team, Professional, Business, Enterprise and Software UI/UX plans

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

An insight alert is displayed when the AI Translation Agent is estimated to outperform the MT engines currently configured for a specific language pair. The alert highlights potential improvements in translation quality and post-editing efficiency based on recent activity data.

The alert appears during [project creation](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10825044285340#UUID-3ec95012-fe40-e725-1b2e-67b8d303676d) after selecting source and target languages, in the Machine translation settings when MT engines are configured for the selected language pair. It is also displayed in the Project details section if the selected [project template](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687) includes an [MT profile](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709660879516#UUID-61be0d13-54d5-e7fd-1fa0-388efceef672 "Phrase Language AI") with engines already configured.

The insight alert is visible to:

- TMS [Administrators](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709753812124#UUID-c512e8a2-c3ee-7321-9b76-81f0dd72517f)
- TMS [Project Managers](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756503196#UUID-f5990e2a-6aa9-ce27-fe52-bdad461726e6) who have access to view all organization data and their own data. PMs with no analytics viewing rights are excluded.

Selecting View comparison opens the Insight side panel, which explains how the recommendation is calculated and presents detailed results per language pair.

###### Comparison logic

The AI Translation Agent (%) indicates the estimated improvement in post-editing performance when replacing the current MT engine setup with the AI Translation Agent. For each language pair:

- Post-edited segments translated with the current MT configuration are analyzed.
- The performance of the current MT engine setup is compared with how the AI Translation Agent typically performs for the same language pair.
- The comparison includes native MT engines, ecosystem MT engines and BYO engines configured within the MT profile.

##### Note

Results are calculated and displayed per language pair. Language locales (e.g., `en-US` vs. `en-GB`) are not considered.

The provided estimates are indicative only and based on historical data and typical AI Translation Agent performance. Actual results may vary.

If multiple MT engines are configured for a language pair:

- A weighted average is calculated across all engines.
- As the AI Translation Agent replaces all engines configured for that language pair, the weighting reflects the actual distribution of editing activity across those engines.

#### AI Translation Agent in the CAT web editor

- AI Translation Agent is available only for batch pre-translation and does not generate real-time suggestions in the CAT web editor. Any suggestions shown in the [CAT pane](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683926812#UUID-fb5d0457-58e7-963b-d4dd-4a069f25139c) are taken from the existing target content.
- AI Translation Agent suggestions are displayed in the same way as standard MT suggestions: the score and origin appear in the relevant column, metadata is shown in the CAT pane.
- AI Translation Agent is listed as an MT provider in the Job resources panel.

#### Language Support

The massively multilingual nature of the Large Language Models (LLM) that power AI Translation Agent makes it capable of addressing many use cases and language needs.

Phrase has identified a subset of language pairs that meet the company’s high-quality standards in both translation directions. While this subset is officially supported, AI Translation Agent is not limited to these language pairs and may be used more broadly.

Recommended language pairs (bidirectional):

- English - Czech
- English - Spanish
- English - French
- English - Russian
- English - German
- English - Italian
- English - Dutch
- English - Chinese Simplified
- English - Swedish
- English - Japanese
- English - Portuguese
- English - Korean
- English - Polish
- English - Danish
- English - Greek
- English - Norwegian (Bokmål)
- English - Indonesian
- English - Hungarian
- English - Arabic
- English - Turkish
- English - Thai

### MT Consumption and Management

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/11530492252444-MT-Consumption-and-Management  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-30T06:01:02Z  
> Labels: Machine Translation, 2BTr, edited_at

Usage of machine translation (MT) services accessed through Phrase Language AI and/or Phrase Portal is measured in Machine Translation Units (MTUs) on [capacity-based plans](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/13872357395228#UUID-77729fa2-a37d-6ee7-c3a0-cba548b0384d), and in credits on [credit-based plans](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/30624146191260#UUID-94ce26d4-ef00-691c-2926-13bbf8448bd1).

##### Monitoring Consumption

Monitor machine translation (MT) usage in the MT usage tab of [Phrase Language AI](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709660879516#UUID-61be0d13-54d5-e7fd-1fa0-388efceef672 "Phrase Language AI").

The tab presents:

- Machine Translation Units (MTUs) and translated characters consumed by [project](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709748435484#UUID-30234837-9796-c6f2-1a21-a623b67ac6cc), [workflow step](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717879324#UUID-3d4fec12-4a9f-ace5-cd20-a1575786a6b7), locale pair, MT engine, and MT profile.
- Comparison of translated content vs. MTU consumption.

- The TMS MT origin filter shows the split of MT units across Phrase TMS:

  - TMS pre-translation
  - TMS analysis
  - TMS CAT editor

  MT units consumed prior to the 1st of December, 2024 are not split by origin and are marked as *Uncategorized*.

##### MT Consumption Calculation

On capacity-based plans, Phrase calculates Machine Translation Units (MTUs) based on the number of input characters sent to the Phrase Language AI service.

On credit-based plans, credits are deducted per input character at the credit rate for the engine type used. Credit rates are shown in Your organization credit rates on the Subscription overview tab of Organization settings.

The number of input characters is calculated based on the number of [Unicode](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Unicode_characters) code units within a string (unlike how calculations are made for [analysis](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709712007708#UUID-7ed2ed16-6064-f667-bb86-7d971d7004c9)).

##### Note

The number of input characters does not directly correspond to the size of an uploaded document or file.

Examples:

- A file with very good translation memory (TM) matches would have a far smaller number of input characters for MT than a file with little to no TM matches.
- If a single file is processed by MT multiple times (once in pre-translation, once in analysis and then again in analysis during a different [workflow step](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717879324#UUID-3d4fec12-4a9f-ace5-cd20-a1575786a6b7)), this would triple the number of input characters.

The charging rates for MTUs depend on the type of engine configured:

- **Native Engines**

  `1 input character = 1 MTU`
- **Ecosystem Engines**

  `1 input character = 0.5 MTU`

  - A fixed fee of 0.5 MTUs per input character for accessing Phrase Language AI (PLAI) services is calculated regardless of how many benefits are used.
  - Benefits of PLAI:

    - Centralized MT profile configuration with custom translation settings and integration across the Phrase Suite.
    - Access to [Phrase Quality Performance Score](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709672289180#UUID-054981cd-9a0e-32bf-79b7-cd6167ebf444) (QPS) for translation quality assessments.
    - Use of custom translation portals for organizational MT needs.
    - Access to [Next MT](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/6638250705436#UUID-cad430d8-49a6-c8e1-7521-ba69e03bdb8a "Phrase NextMT") and [Next GenMT](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/14299433827996#UUID-46f178b8-f25f-16a6-1e53-465602f9e71a "Phrase Next GenMT") (LLM-based) engines.
    - Support for custom-trained engines via [Custom AI](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/7683093364508#UUID-7e94a40d-cd95-9388-1d29-cb05cb65a32f).
- **Bring Your Own Engines (BYO Engines)**

  `1 input character = 0.5 MTU`

##### Note

Depending on MT engine and tag display, [segments](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709678012828#UUID-753af50b-27d0-e386-418a-452ed30e0083) containing [tags](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709695024412#UUID-9f36c0c5-d7f2-0f81-ec55-44efc63d0050) may consume MTUs/credits.

##### Consumption Management

The number of MTUs/credits consumed depends on configuration choices and content type. In Phrase TMS, adjusting the settings for pre-translation, analysis and the CAT editor can optimize MT consumption.

- [Pre-translation](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717749788#UUID-a982e5f9-9aa2-434f-116c-17b822d35343)

  A [TM threshold](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/16057517629852#UUID-22373687-0c7d-80bf-7a0a-f0fd7a3508b0) sets when pre-translation uses MT and as MT has lower editing times, higher TM thresholds reduce human post-editing efforts. Pre-translation only uses MT if no [TM](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75) match is found at or above the set threshold. Using the recommended threshold is likely to reduce total translation costs.

  - In the global Pre-translation settings or for a specific project or project template, ensure Apply MT only when there's no TM match at or above the threshold is enabled.

  Example scenarios:

  - Documentation with strong TM leverage (e.g., technical documentation, structured or repetitive text)

    MT usage is minimized to rely on TM matches. Pre-translating with a lower TM threshold is recommended.
  - Marketing or creative content (e.g., campaign copy, product descriptions, website text)

    MT usage is acceptable to reduce post-editing effort and improve fluency. Pre-translating with a higher TM threshold is recommended.

  ### Tip

  TM penalties can lower matches below the set threshold, which may cause MT to be called unnecessarily. Adjust penalties and thresholds together to control MT usage.

  Higher thresholds result in more MT usage and more MTUs/credits (because fewer segments are covered by TM and MT is triggered more often).

  Lower thresholds result in less MT usage and fewer MTUs/credits (more segments are satisfied by TM and MT is skipped).

  | MT enabled in Pre-translation | Apply MT only when there’s no TM ≥ threshold | Result | MT consumption |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | Off | Off | Pre-translation never calls MT. | No MTUs/credits consumed. |
  | Off | On | Pre-translation never calls MT. | No MTUs/credits consumed. |
  | On | Off | MT can be applied regardless of TM matches (no threshold check). | MTUs/credits consumed for segments where MT is applied |
  | On | On | MT is applied only when there’s no TM match ≥ threshold. | Reduced MTUs/credits: only segments below the threshold call MT |
- [Default analysis](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709712007708#UUID-7ed2ed16-6064-f667-bb86-7d971d7004c9)

  The standard analysis is run on uploaded source segments before translation to assess translation effort and serve as the baseline for generating [quotes](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709635877020#UUID-317de099-f8f9-9c94-dde1-36018cb96947) for clients.

  MT is only called if a [quality performance score](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709672289180#UUID-054981cd-9a0e-32bf-79b7-cd6167ebf444) (QPS) is required and if there are no 100/101% TM or non-translatables (NT) matches.

  If not required:

  - Disable the Include machine translation suggestions (QPS) option.
  - Ensure 100/101% TM or non-translatables (NT) matches are prioritized over MT matches in pre-translation settings.

  ### Note

  Post-editing analysis does not consume MTUs/credits.

  Turning QPS off is the only way to make sure analysis never consumes MTUs/credits.

  Keeping QPS on but combining it with Apply MT only reduces unnecessary MT queries if MT leverage needs to be seen in analysis.

  | QPS (Include MT matches) | Apply MT only when no TM ≥ threshold | MT consumption in Analysis |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | Off | Off | No MTUs/credits consumed (MT not queried at all) |
  | Off | On | No MTUs/credits consumed (QPS is off → Apply MT setting has no effect in Analysis) |
  | On | Off | MTUs/credits consumed for all segments without 100/101% TM/NT matches (even if fuzzy TM matches exist, MT still queried) |
  | On | On | Reduced MTU/credit consumption → MTUs/credits consumed only for segments with no TM ≥ threshold; segments with 100/101% or other high TM matches are excluded from MT queries |
- [CAT editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683847964#UUID-7174fb87-7107-2f32-ce55-5e040de5446c)

  - In the CAT editor settings for the project or project template, enable Suggest MT only for segments with a TM match below xxx%.

    - Enabling this option controls when MT is suggested in the CAT pane. MT will only be used for the segments with a TM match below the set threshold.
    - If segments are confirmed (and thus saved to TM) in a workflow step, they will have a 101% TM match in subsequent steps. As a result, MT will not be suggested, preserving MTUs/credits in those steps.
    - If this option is disabled, MT is suggested regardless of the threshold, leading to MTU/credit consumption.
  - A further optimization of MTU/credit consumption can be achieved by [pre-translating](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709717749788#UUID-a982e5f9-9aa2-434f-116c-17b822d35343) a job before opening it in the editor.

    If MT is used as the target during pre-translation, the [CAT pane](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683926812#UUID-fb5d0457-58e7-963b-d4dd-4a069f25139c) reuses the MT suggestion without making a new MT request. The reused suggestion is carried through all workflow steps without calling MT again.

    This optimization is enabled by default (without any settings control).

    ### Note

    This optimization is not available in the [CAT desktop editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683873052#UUID-92b9886a-e3a5-6975-8c35-246200ac54ef).

    MTUs/credits are still consumed in the following cases:

    - No pre-translation is performed on the job.
    - MT is not used for a segment during pre-translation (e.g., NT is used instead).
    - A TM match is used in pre-translation, and MT is called if the TM match does not meet the threshold defined for the CAT editor.
    - The source text is changed, or a segment is joined/split.
    - The MT engine is changed after pre-translation.
    - The source text is automatically updated during a source update or as part of a [continuous job](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709711922972#UUID-ec063437-ae86-bb7b-9358-19bb3087a3c5).

    Higher thresholds result in more MT usage and more MTUs/credits (because fewer segments are covered by TM and thus MT is triggered more often).

    Lower thresholds result in less MT usage and fewer MTUs/credits (more segments are satisfied by TM and skip MT).
  - MTUs/credits are consumed both when working on [segments](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709678012828#UUID-753af50b-27d0-e386-418a-452ed30e0083) within the editor and when segments are pre-loaded:

    - CAT results are pre-loaded to minimize wait times, consuming MTUs/credits for the previous segment, the current segment, and the next two segments.
    - When navigating with keyboard arrows or using Go to segment, MTUs/credits are consumed for these pre-loaded segments.
    - When jumping upon confirmation, MTUs/credits are consumed for the next two segments based on the After confirming a segment, jump to setting in the editor.
    - Pre-loading respects [filtering](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709720416796#UUID-11936659-047d-2507-3e91-ab2208f2ef73) results, ensuring that MTUs/credits are consumed only for segments displayed after filtering.
  - MTUs/credits are not consumed repeatedly for revisited segments within the same user session. A user session remains active as long as the job is open in the browser.

    - By default, the maximum editor user session lasts three days (unless a shorter session duration is set in TMS). After this period, the user is required to refresh the job.
    - Refreshing the job, whether due to session expiration or manual refresh, ends the current user session.
    - Upon refreshing, a new user session begins, and MTUs/credits are consumed again for the relevant segments.
  - User role rights

    - MT can be enabled or disabled, and MTU/credit consumption further optimized, for [Linguist](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709735838492#UUID-911ee8d4-ecc1-0c38-8f71-7c22f1646545) and [Guest](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709756557724#UUID-9cdd9c56-fc67-1d5f-7cf0-7fbfe3a993b9) users in their user profile.

  Threshold ON ensures MT is only called if TM is too weak (< threshold).

  Pre-translation with MT → saves MTUs/credits, as the editor reuses MT suggestions (web editor only).

  Pre-translation with strong TM (≥ threshold) → no MTUs/credits used.

  Pre-translation with weak TM (< threshold) → MT still queried in CAT, consuming MTUs/credits.

  | Suggest MT only for segments below threshold | Pre-translation situation | CAT editor suggestions (CAT pane) | MT consumption |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | Off | No pre-translation | MT is suggested for all segments, regardless of TM matches. | MTUs/credits consumed |
  | Off | Segment pre-translated with MT | CAT reuses MT suggestions from pre-translation, no new MT calls. | No MTUs/credits consumed |
  | On | No pre-translation | MT is suggested only for segments with TM below threshold. | Reduced MTU/credit consumption |
  | On | Segment pre-translated with MT | CAT reuses MT suggestions from pre-translation, no new MT calls. | No MTUs/credits consumed |
  | On | Segment pre-translated with TM ≥ threshold | MT not suggested (TM covers it). | No MTUs/credits consumed |
  | On | Segment pre-translated with TM ≥ threshold | MT is still called in the editor, because TM did not meet the threshold. | MTUs/credits consumed |
- [Phrase Portal](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/12949538303516#UUID-0af9ae02-cbd1-1c17-aec5-541956f27282)

  MT is always used, and third-party engines outside PLAI cannot be configured.

  - Enabling Quality Performance Score (QPS) in a Phrase Portal does not add extra MTU/credit consumption.

  - To reduce MTU consumption, use an ecosystem engine with credentials to consume only 0.5 MTU per character.
- [Phrase Strings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5821202515996#UUID-3f860272-82b6-b342-44a0-a97106ae0685)

  When Phrase Language AI is selected as machine translation provider, MTUs/credits from the Phrase Platform subscription allowance/credit pool are always consumed.

  - To reduce MTU/credit consumption, use any other 3rd party MT engine available in Phrase Strings.
- [Phrase Studio](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/21177186715420#UUID-df5d31ff-16d9-6310-bc96-0818fca7a7d9)

  When Phrase Language AI is selected as translation AI model, MTUs/credits from the Phrase Platform subscription allowance/credit pool are always consumed.

  - To reduce MTU consumption, use any other translation AI model available in Phrase Studio.

##### MT Consumption Across Shared Projects and Jobs

MT consumption is tracked at the buyer organization regardless of which party triggers it. When a project involves a vendor, MT usage triggered by the vendor draws from the buyer organization's MTU allowance or credit pool.

A vendor running independent pre-translation, analysis, or CAT editor activity with its own settings consumes MTUs/credits from the buyer organization, even if the buyer uses different default settings.

The project or project template "Suggest MT only for segments with a TM match below xxx%" setting takes precedence over the individual "Enable MT" toggle in a user profile for automatic MT suggestions in the CAT editor. The "Enable MT" toggle only controls whether a Linguist or Guest user can manually look up or insert an MT suggestion. It does not control the CAT editor's automatic background MT calls, which follow the project or template setting regardless of the user's "Enable MT" toggle state.

Changes made to pre-translation, analysis, or CAT editor MT settings at the project or project template level apply only to jobs created after the change. They do not apply retroactively to jobs that already exist. Jobs created before a settings change continue to use the original settings until the job is completed or the settings are manually reapplied on that job. To apply a settings correction to jobs already in progress, reapply the project or template CAT editor settings directly on each affected job.

---

## Term Bases

Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/sections/10112300098204-Term-Bases

### Term Bases Overview

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733372188-Term-Bases-Overview  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2025-04-07T09:48:58Z  
> Labels: Linguist, Project Manager, Term Bases, 2BTr, ar_tms, tb

A term base (also known as a translation glossary) is a terminology database that stores approved terms and their translations. Storing terminology helps keep translations consistent within projects and ensures translators use correct designated terms.

Unlike [translation memories](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709688865692#UUID-c58dd835-39ea-1a94-02d1-9f2517fd6d75) (TM), which store full segments or phrases, term bases (TB) are concept-based (grouping terms by meaning) and focus on single words; a single term in a language can have one or more counterparts in other languages. The term base module can support multiple languages in a single term base.

When working in the translation editor, the system scans the source content and automatically suggests the correct translation for terms matching an entry in the assigned term base.

Using a term base is especially valuable in projects involving:

- Specialized or technical vocabulary
- Brand-specific language
- Terms that should remain untranslated
- Words with different meanings depending on the context
- Multiple translators or external vendors, where consistent terminology is critical

Explore [best practices for using a term base](https://phrase.com/blog/posts/term-base/).

#### Term Bases in Phrase TMS

- Term bases can be multilingual and can be assigned to multiple projects.
- Specified [Linguists or Vendors](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5793349215900#UUID-09f99b48-09c7-b144-42bb-a1bfd2e7d452) may have unlimited editing rights for any term.
- Term bases can be accessed and edited by Guest users (clients) if such a role is available in the selected [subscription plan](https://phrase.com/pricing/).
- A maximum of 10 term bases can be assigned to project/language pair.
- Existing term bases can be [imported or exported](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733407772#UUID-86eb21b6-c9e1-eba5-a2ed-b2dd4ca4ce39 "Modify or Import to Term Bases (TMS)").

Term bases can be assigned to projects in the following modes:

- **Read**: Checks if terms are found in the source text, if so they are returned on the [CAT pane](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683926812#UUID-fb5d0457-58e7-963b-d4dd-4a069f25139c) of the editor.
- **Write**: Terms can be [added and edited](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709683926812#UUID-fb5d0457-58e7-963b-d4dd-4a069f25139c) (with limits) by linguists during translation. New terms are not automatically applied to an entire translation project and would have to be manually updated (search and replace) to ensure translation accuracy.
- **QA**: As part of [quality assurance](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709703799324#UUID-83632035-0d65-f957-b0dd-ad00704f8d25), it is checked if terms with *Approved* status in the source have a corresponding term translation (*New* or *Approved* status) in the target.

#### Term Bases in Phrase Strings

#### Available for

- Advanced and Enterprise plan (Legacy)

Get in touch with [Sales](https://phrase.com/demo/) for licensing questions.

- Terms are first added to a term base, the term base is then [uploaded to Strings](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10112307427740#UUID-9d922c4d-a12d-4600-3417-0cbd17c56700 "Uploading and Downloading Terms (Strings)") by file upload (.csv).
- Term bases can be assigned, as well as unassigned, to *all* or *selected* [spaces](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784094645916#UUID-af3e73fe-7d27-380b-cd28-f802c33e621b) and/or [projects](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5784094677404#UUID-67ae68da-d206-0281-f708-35cfdfb52252).
- Once a term base is assigned, matching terms from the term base will be highlighted in the source and target language of the [translation editor](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5822638157340#UUID-52f5cddb-2b75-2ffe-3f95-87cc2b002bfa)
- Terms will only be suggested for strictly matching [locales](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5818281650204#UUID-ecfff114-7e61-c99d-f630-6f7ceca104f8), i.e. regional variants of a language (en-US, es-MX, etc.).

### Term Bases (TMS)

Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/sections/10112323675292-Term-Bases-TMS

#### Term Morphology (TMS)

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733389084-Term-Morphology-TMS  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-06-17T10:28:13Z  
> Labels: Term Bases, 2BTr, ar_tms

Term morphology is handled several ways:

- Fuzzy matches
- Exact matches
- Word stem separation
- Synonym use

##### Fuzzy Match Type

By default, the match type for new terms is set to *Fuzzy*.

When the match type is Fuzzy, a suffix can be appended to a word and it will still be matched.

**Examples:**

The simple morphology of added characters.

- *car* will also match *cars*
- *game* will also match *games*

Maximum suffix length is 6 for [fusional](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fusional_language) languages and 20 for [agglutinative](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Agglutinative_language) languages.

##### Exact Match Type

Fuzzy match might not be suitable for words such as *contract* as *contractor* would be an incorrect match. This term would be better served with *Exact match* settings

Exact match is also recommended for abbreviations; *AN* may require an exact match and also be *Case sensitive*. Only *AN* would provide and exact match while *an*, *and* or *AND* would not.

Any term that exclusively consists of special characters (anything that isn't a Unicode letter or mark, excepting |) is automatically marked as exact match. Fuzzy matching doesn’t handle these special characters well which leads to unreliable or incorrect matches.

##### Terms with Rich Morphology

To improve matching for terms with rich [morphology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morphology_(linguistics)) as when part of a word changes due to context, a boundary between the word stem (the part that does not change) and the suffix (the part that does change) can be defined by inserting a pipe character `|`.

Fuzzy terms are recognized up to a maximum of 6 characters after the pipe character.

**Examples:**

*biolog|ic* will also match *biolog|y*, *biolog|ical* and *biologic|s*.

*educat|e* will also match *educat|ion*, *educat|ional* and *educat|ed*.

##### Synonyms

For some terms, the fuzzy match or separating stem will not work; *leaf* and *leaves* would not match in one term. In such case, use synonyms.

**Example:**

Create the term *contract* and its synonym *contracts* and set them both to Exact match. This recognizes both forms of the word while ignoring *contractor* that would be recognized with a Fuzzy match.

Click through [tutorial](https://phrase.navattic.com/022505af) on working with term morphology.

---

#### Term Base Page (TMS)

> Quelle: https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/10112323818908-Term-Base-Page-TMS  
> Zuletzt aktualisiert: 2026-09-29T06:25:52Z  
> Labels: Term Bases, 2BTr, tb, edited_at

Clicking on a TB from the Term bases panel or a project page opens the term base page.

- New terms (not approved) are displayed with grey status.
- Forbidden terms are indicated with a checkmark ![Forbidden term](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682507790364) in the Forbidden column.
- The term concept (source term, target term, synonyms) is displayed as a block of rows distinguished with white and grey background colors.
- System-defined term attributes and [user-defined custom fields](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/22507164182812#UUID-49eb4734-0781-2aab-7a27-1f2dda3169f8 "Custom Fields for Concepts and Terms (TMS)") (if any) are displayed in columns that can be customized by clicking Customize.

Dependent on user rights:

- The attributes of a term base can be edited.
- A TB can be deleted. This moves the TB to the [recycle bin](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709704092572#UUID-1e37e39c-e4a8-3f6b-fd8f-4a1e11ed0265) where it will remain for 30 days.
- Content can be searched for individual entries requiring editing. [Wildcards](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709733449372#UUID-2a4c8798-761f-051a-255c-f1faf84196e6) are supported and the results can be filtered. New searches reset the filter.
- Content can be imported from other TBs or other CAT tools.
- Content of a TB can be exported for editing in another CAT tool and imported back (options are in the Actions ![More Menu](https://support.phrase.com/hc/article_attachments/30682546302492) menu at the top of the page).
- Terms can be added, deleted and edited.

The Related projects table presents all projects a specific TB is associated with.

###### Note

Once added, target languages cannot be removed from a TB as long as:

- There is a single entry in the TB (even if only in the other target languages).
- The TB is used in an existing project.
- The TB is used in any [project template](https://support.phrase.com/hc/en-us/articles/5709647439772#UUID-94346683-ae2a-16b5-7e16-94a554612687).

**Managing Languages in a Term Base**

Languages can be added to an existing term base at any time via Edit term base. Adding languages always succeeds. A language can only be removed from a term base when all of the following conditions are true:

- The term base contains no terms in any language. Empty concepts (concepts with no terms attached) do not count as a blocker.
- The term base is not assigned to any project, regardless of that project's status. Completed and cancelled projects count.
- The term base is not used in any project template.

When the conditions are met, the language is moved to the Inactive list in the Edit dialog and the term base is saved with Update term base. If removal is blocked, the save fails with the error: "Languages can only be removed from an empty term base which isn't assigned to a project or project template." If several languages are removed at once and the check fails for any of them, none are removed.

If removal is not possible, the recommended workaround is to export the term base as an XLSX file, create a new term base configured with only the languages to keep, and import the file into it. The new term base then needs to be reassigned to the relevant projects and templates.

**Note**: adding `|delete` to a TID in an exported XLSX removes the terms for a specific language but does not remove the language itself from the term base.
